To*get, decode, and split*a [header value](#content-type-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")
value, run these steps. They return a [list](#x-content-type-options-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") of[strings](#x-content-type-options-header "Content Security Policy Level 3").
   

     1.       Let input be the result of [isomorphic decoding](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3") value. 
     
2.       Let position be a [position variable](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") for input, initially pointing at the start of input. 
     
3.       Let values be a [list](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3") of [strings](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3"), initially « ». 
     
4.       Let temporaryValue be the empty string. 
     
5.       While true:  
     

       1.         Append the result of [collecting a sequence of code points](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") that are not U+0022 (") or    U+002C (,) from input, given position, to temporaryValue.    
        
	The result might be the empty string.   
       
2.         If position is not past the end of input and the    [code point](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") at position within input is U+0022 ("):    
       

         1.           Append the result of [collecting an HTTP quoted string](#cors-preflight-cache "Content Security Policy Level 3") from input,     given position, to temporaryValue.     
         
2. If position is not past the end of input, then     [continue](#tao-check "Content Security Policy Level 3").            

       
-         Remove all [HTTP tab or space](#deferred-fetch-quota "Content Security Policy Level 3") from the start and end of temporaryValue.   
       
-         [Append](#fetch-api "Content Security Policy Level 3") temporaryValue to values.   
       
-         Set temporaryValue to the empty string.   
       
-         If position is past the end of input, then return values.   
       
-         [Assert](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3"): the [code point](#request-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") at position within   input is U+002C (,).   
       
-         Advance position by 1.  
      

    

    
Except for blessed call sites, the algorithm directly above is not to be invokeddirectly. 
Use [get, decode, and split](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3") instead.
      

    
To *append* a [header](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3")(name, 
value) to a [header list](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3") list:
   

     1.       If list [contains](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level 3") name, then set name  to the first such [header](#websocket-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [name](#fetch-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3").  
      
	This reuses the casing of the [name](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3") of the [header](#fetch-elsewhere-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")  already in list, if any. If there are multiple matched [headers](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3") their  [names](#acknowledgments "Content Security Policy Level 3") will all be identical. 
     
2.       [Append](#ipr "Content Security Policy Level 3") (name, value) to list.
    

      

    
To *delete* a[header name](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") name from 
a [header list](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") list,[remove](#normative "Content Security Policy Level 3") all [headers](#informative "Content Security Policy Level 3") whose [name](#idl-index "Content Security Policy Level 3") is a[byte-case-insensitive](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") match for
name from list.
      

    
To *set* a [header](#biblio-fetch-metadata "Content Security Policy Level 3")(name, 
value) in a [header list](#biblio-sw "Fetch Metadata Request Headers") list:
   

     1.       If list [contains](#biblio-mix "Mixed Content") name, then set the [value](#biblio-upgrade-insecure-requests "Upgrade Insecure Requests") of the first such [header](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css) to value and [remove](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css) the others. 
     
2.       Otherwise, [append](h-entry "subtitle") (name, value) to list.
    

      

    
To *combine* a[header](h-entry "subtitle") (name, value) in a [header list](h-entry "subtitle")list:
   

     1.       If list [contains](h-entry "subtitle") name, then set the [value](h-entry "subtitle") of the first such [header](h-entry "subtitle") to its [value](h-entry "subtitle"), followed by 0x2C 0x20, followed by value. 
     
2.       Otherwise, [append](h-entry "subtitle") (name, value) to list.
    

    
[Combine](h-entry "subtitle") is used by `[XMLHttpRequest](h-entry "subtitle")` and the[WebSocket protocol handshake](h-entry "subtitle").
      

    
To *convert header names to a sorted-lowercase set*, given a [list](h-entry "subtitle") of[names](h-entry "subtitle")
headerNames, run these steps. They return an[ordered set](h-entry "subtitle") of [header names](h-entry "subtitle").
   

     1.       Let headerNamesSet be a new [ordered set](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
2.       [For each](h-entry "subtitle") name of headerNames, [append](h-entry "subtitle") the result of [byte-lowercasing](h-entry "subtitle") name to headerNamesSet. 
     
3.       Return the result of [sorting](h-entry "subtitle") headerNamesSet in ascending order with [byte less than](h-entry "subtitle").
    

      

    
To *sort and combine* a[header list](h-entry "subtitle") list, run these steps. They 
return a [header list](h-entry "subtitle").
   

     1.       Let headers be a [header list](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
2.       Let names be the result of [convert header names to a sorted-lowercase set](h-entry "subtitle") with all the [names](h-entry "subtitle") of the [headers](h-entry "subtitle") in list. 
     
3.       [For each](h-entry "subtitle") name of names:  
     

       1.         If name is \``set-cookie`\`, then:    
       

         1.           Let values be a list of all [values](h-entry "subtitle") of     [headers](h-entry "subtitle") in list whose [name](h-entry "subtitle") is a     [byte-case-insensitive](h-entry "subtitle") match for name, in order.     
         
2.           [For each](h-entry "subtitle") value of values:      
         

           1.             [Append](h-entry "subtitle") (name, value) to headers.      
          

        

       
-         Otherwise:    
       

         1.           Let value be the result of [getting](h-entry "subtitle") name     from list.     
         
2.           [Assert](h-entry "subtitle"): value is non-null.     
         
3.           [Append](h-entry "subtitle") (name, value) to headers.    
        

      

     
-       Return headers.
    

      
---
   
A *header* is a [tuple](h-entry "subtitle") that consists of a*name* (a [header name](h-entry "subtitle"))
and*value* (a [header value](h-entry "subtitle")).
   
A *header name* is a [byte sequence](h-entry "subtitle") that matches the[field-name](h-entry "subtitle") token production.
   
A *header value* is a [byte sequence](h-entry "subtitle") that matches the followingconditions:
   
    -      Has no leading or trailing [HTTP tab or space bytes](h-entry "subtitle"). 
    
-      Contains no 0x00 (NUL) or [HTTP newline bytes](h-entry "subtitle").
   
   
The definition of [header value](h-entry "subtitle") is not defined in terms of the[field-value](h-entry "subtitle") token production as it is[not compatible with deployed content](h-entry "subtitle").
   

    
To *normalize* a[byte sequence](h-entry "subtitle") potentialValue, remove any leading 
and trailing[HTTP whitespace bytes](h-entry "subtitle") from potentialValue.
      
---
   

    
To determine whether a [header](h-entry "subtitle") (name, value)is a *CORS-safelisted 
request-header*, run these steps:
   

     1.       If value’s [length](h-entry "subtitle") is greater than 128, then return false. 
     
2.       [Byte-lowercase](h-entry "subtitle") name and switch on the result:  
             \``accept`\`                  If value contains a [CORS-unsafe request-header byte](h-entry "subtitle"), then return false.   
       \``accept-language`\`          \``content-language`\`                  If value contains a byte that is not in the range 0x30 (0) to 0x39 (9),   inclusive, is not in the range 0x41 (A) to 0x5A (Z), inclusive, is not in the range 0x61 (a) to   0x7A (z), inclusive, and is not 0x20 (SP), 0x2A (\*), 0x2C (,), 0x2D (-), 0x2E (.), 0x3B (;), or   0x3D (=), then return false.   
       \``content-type`\`                 

         1.           If value contains a [CORS-unsafe request-header byte](h-entry "subtitle"), then return     false.     
         
2.           Let mimeType be the result of [parsing](h-entry "subtitle") the     result of [isomorphic decoding](h-entry "subtitle") value.     
         
3.           If mimeType is failure, then return false.     
         
4.           If mimeType’s [essence](h-entry "subtitle") is not     "`application/x-www-form-urlencoded`", "`multipart/form-data`", or     "`text/plain`", then return false.    
        

        This intentionally does not use [extract a MIME type](h-entry "subtitle") as that algorithm is    rather forgiving and servers are not expected to implement it.    
        

                       If [extract a MIME type](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") were used the following request would not result in a CORS     preflight and a naïve parser on the server might treat the request body as JSON:     

		fetch("https://victim.example/naïve-endpoint", {
  method: "POST",
  headers: [
    ["Content-Type", "application/json"],
    ["Content-Type", "text/plain"]
  ],
  credentials: "include",
  body: JSON.stringify(exerciseForTheReader)
});
               \``range`\`                 

         1.           Let rangeValue be the result of [parsing a single range header value](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")     given value and false.     
         
2.           If rangeValue is failure, then return false.     
         
3.           If rangeValue[0] is null, then return false.      
          
	As web browsers have historically not emitted ranges such as      \``bytes=-500`\` this algorithm does not safelist them.    
        

       Otherwise                  Return false.  
           
-       Return true.
    

    
There are limited exceptions to the \``Content-Type`\` header safelist, asdocumented in [CORS protocol exceptions](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").
      

    
A *CORS-unsafe request-header byte* is a byte byte for which one of thefollowing 
is true:
    
     -       byte is less than 0x20 and is not 0x09 HT 
     
-       byte is 0x22 ("), 0x28 (left parenthesis), 0x29 (right parenthesis), 0x3A (:), 0x3C (<), 0x3E (>), 0x3F (?), 0x40 (@), 0x5B ([), 0x5C (\\), 0x5D (]), 0x7B ({), 0x7D (}), or 0x7F DEL.
    
      

    
The *CORS-unsafe request-header names*, given a [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css
"subtitle")headers, are determined as follows:
   

     1.       Let unsafeNames be a new [list](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"). 
     
2.       Let potentiallyUnsafeNames be a new [list](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"). 
     
3.       Let safelistValueSize be 0. 
     
4.       [For each](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") header of headers:  
     

       1.         If header is not a [CORS-safelisted request-header](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then   [append](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") header’s [name](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to unsafeNames.   
       
2.         Otherwise, [append](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") header’s [name](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to   potentiallyUnsafeNames and increase safelistValueSize by   header’s [value](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [length](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").  
      

     
-       If safelistValueSize is greater than 1024, then [for each](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") name of potentiallyUnsafeNames, [append](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") name to unsafeNames. 
     
-       Return the result of [convert header names to a sorted-lowercase set](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") with unsafeNames.
    

      
A *CORS non-wildcard request-header name* is a [header name](# "subtitle") that is a[byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for
\``Authorization`\`.
   
A *privileged no-CORS request-header name* is a [header name](# "subtitle") that isa [byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for one of
   
    - \``Range`\`.   
   

    
These are headers that can be set by privileged APIs, and will be preserved if their 
associated request object is copied, but will be removed if the request is modified 
by unprivileged APIs. 
    
\``Range`\` headers are commonly used by [downloads](# "subtitle") and [media fetches](# "subtitle"). 
    
A helper is provided to [add a range header](# "subtitle") to a particular request.
      
A *CORS-safelisted response-header name*, given a [list](# "subtitle") of[header names](# "subtitle")
list, is a [header name](# "subtitle") that is a[byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for one of
   
    - \``Cache-Control`\`     
- \``Content-Language`\`     
- \``Content-Length`\`     
- \``Content-Type`\`     
- \``Expires`\`     
- \``Last-Modified`\`     
- \``Pragma`\`     
- Any [item](# "subtitle") in list that is not a [forbidden response-header name](# "subtitle").   
   
A *no-CORS-safelisted request-header name* is a [header name](# "subtitle") thatis a [byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for one of
   
    - \``Accept`\`     
- \``Accept-Language`\`     
- \``Content-Language`\`     
- \``Content-Type`\`   
   

    
To determine whether a [header](# "subtitle") (name, value) is a*no-CORS-safelisted 
request-header*, run these steps:
   

     1.       If name is not a [no-CORS-safelisted request-header name](# "subtitle"), then return false. 
     
2.       Return whether (name, value) is a [CORS-safelisted request-header](# "subtitle").
    

      

    
A [header](# "subtitle") (name, value) is*forbidden request-header* if these steps 
return true:
   

     1.       If name is a [byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for one of:  
             2. \``Accept-Charset`\`          
3. \``Accept-Encoding`\`          
4. \`[`Access-Control-Request-Headers`](# "subtitle")\`          
5. \`[`Access-Control-Request-Method`](# "subtitle")\`          
6. \``Connection`\`          
7. \``Content-Length`\`          
8. \``Cookie`\`          
9. \``Cookie2`\`          
10. \``Date`\`          
11. \``DNT`\`          
12. \``Expect`\`          
13. \``Host`\`          
14. \``Keep-Alive`\`          
15. \`[`Origin`](# "subtitle")\`          
16. \``Referer`\`          
17. \``Set-Cookie`\`          
18. \``TE`\`          
19. \``Trailer`\`          
20. \``Transfer-Encoding`\`          
21. \``Upgrade`\`          
22. \``Via`\`        
      
then return true. 
     
23.       
If name when [byte-lowercased](# "subtitle") [starts with](# "subtitle") \``proxy-`\` or 
\``sec-`\`, then return true. 
     
24.       
If name is a [byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for one of:  
      
       25. \``X-HTTP-Method`\`          
26. \``X-HTTP-Method-Override`\`          
27. \``X-Method-Override`\`        
      
then:  
     

       1.         Let parsedValues be the result of   [getting, decoding, and splitting](# "subtitle") value.   
       
2.         [For each](# "subtitle") method of parsedValues: if the   [isomorphic encoding](# "subtitle") of method is a [forbidden method](# "subtitle"), then return true.  
      

     
-       
Return false.
    

    

     
These are forbidden so the user agent remains in full control over them. 
     
[Header names](# "subtitle") starting with \``Sec-`\` are reserved to allow new [headers](# "subtitle") to be minted that are safe from APIs using [fetch](# "subtitle") that allow control over [headers](# "subtitle") by developers, such as `[XMLHttpRequest](#
"subtitle")`. [[XHR]](# "subtitle") 
     
The \``Set-Cookie`\` header is semantically a response header, so it is not useful on requests. 
Because \``Set-Cookie`\` headers cannot be combined, they require more complex handling in 
the `[Headers](# "subtitle")` object. It is forbidden here to avoid leaking this complexity 
into requests.
          
A *forbidden response-header name* is a [header name](# "subtitle") that is a[byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for one of:
   
    - \``Set-Cookie`\`     
- \``Set-Cookie2`\`   
   
A *request-body-header name* is a [header name](# "subtitle") that is a[byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for one of:
   
    - \``Content-Encoding`\`     
- \``Content-Language`\`     
- \``Content-Location`\`     
- \``Content-Type`\`   
   
---
   

    
To *extract header values*given a [header](# "subtitle") header, run these steps:
   

     1.       If parsing header’s [value](# "subtitle"), per the [ABNF](# "subtitle") for header’s [name](# "subtitle"), fails, then return failure. 
     
2.       Return one or more [values](# "subtitle") resulting from parsing header’s [value](# "subtitle"), per the [ABNF](# "subtitle") for header’s [name](# "subtitle").
    

      

    
To*extract header list values*given a [header name](# "subtitle") name and a [header list](# "subtitle")
list,run these steps:
   

     1.       If list [does not contain](# "subtitle") name, then return null. 
     
2.       If the [ABNF](# "subtitle") for name allows a single [header](# "subtitle") and list  [contains](# "subtitle") more than one, then return failure.  
      
	If different error handling is needed, extract the desired [header](# "subtitle")  first. 
     
3.       Let values be an empty [list](# "subtitle"). 
     
4.       For each [header](# "subtitle") header list  [contains](# "subtitle") whose [name](# "subtitle") is name:  
     

       1.         Let extract be the result of [extracting header values](# "subtitle") from   header.   
       
2.         If extract is failure, then return failure.   
       
3.         Append each [value](# "subtitle") in extract, in order, to values.  
      

     
-       Return values.
    

      

    
To *build a content range* given an integer rangeStart, an integerrangeEnd, and an 
integer fullLength, run these steps:
   

     1.       Let contentRange be \``bytes `\`. 
     
2.       Append rangeStart, [serialized](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") and [isomorphic encoded](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"), to contentRange. 
     
3.       Append 0x2D (-) to contentRange. 
     
4.       Append rangeEnd, [serialized](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") and [isomorphic encoded](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") to contentRange. 
     
5.       Append 0x2F (/) to contentRange. 
     
6.       Append fullLength, [serialized](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") and [isomorphic encoded](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") to contentRange. 
     
7.       Return contentRange.
    

      

    
To *parse a single range header value* from a[byte sequence](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")
value and a boolean allowWhitespace, run these steps:
   

     1.       Let data be the [isomorphic decoding](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") of value. 
     
2.       If data does not [start with](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") "`bytes`", then return failure. 
     
3.       Let position be a [position variable](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") for data, initially pointing at the 5th [code point](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") of data. 
     
4.       If allowWhitespace is true, [collect a sequence of code points](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") that are [HTTP tab or space](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"), from data given position. 
     
5.       If the [code point](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") at position within data is not U+003D (=), then return failure. 
     
6.       Advance position by 1. 
     
7.       If allowWhitespace is true, [collect a sequence of code points](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") that are [HTTP tab or space](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle"), from data given position. 
     
8.       Let rangeStart be the result of [collecting a sequence of code points](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") that are [ASCII digits](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle"), from data given position. 
     
9.       Let rangeStartValue be rangeStart, interpreted as decimal number, if rangeStart is not the empty string; otherwise null. 
     
10.       If allowWhitespace is true, [collect a sequence of code points](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") that are [HTTP tab or space](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle"), from data given position. 
     
11.       If the [code point](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") at position within data is not U+002D (-), then return failure. 
     
12.       Advance position by 1. 
     
13.       If allowWhitespace is true, [collect a sequence of code points](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") that are [HTTP tab or space](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle"), from data given position. 
     
14.       Let rangeEnd be the result of [collecting a sequence of code points](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") that are [ASCII digits](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle"), from data given position. 
     
15.       Let rangeEndValue be rangeEnd, interpreted as decimal number, if rangeEnd is not the empty string; otherwise null. 
     
16.       If position is not past the end of data, then return failure. 
     
17.       If rangeEndValue and rangeStartValue are null, then return failure. 
     
18.       If rangeStartValue and rangeEndValue are numbers, and rangeStartValue is greater than rangeEndValue, then return failure. 
     
19.       Return (rangeStartValue, rangeEndValue).  
      
	The range end or start can be omitted, e.g., \``bytes=0-`\` or  \``bytes=-500`\` are valid ranges.
    

    
[Parse a single range header value](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") succeeds for a subset of allowed range headervalues, but it is the most common form used by user agents when requesting media or resumingdownloads. This format of range header value can be set using [add a range header](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
      
---
   
A *default \``User-Agent`\` value* is an[implementation-defined](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [header value](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for the
\``User-Agent`\`[header](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
For unfortunate web compatibility reasons, web browsers are strongly encouraged tohave 
this value start with \``Mozilla/5.0 (`\` and be generally modeled after other webbrowsers.
   

    
To get the [environment settings object](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") environment’s*environment 
default \``User-Agent`\` value*:
   

     1.       Let userAgent be the [WebDriver BiDi emulated User-Agent](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for environment. 
     
2.       If userAgent is non-null, then return userAgent, [isomorphic encoded](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). 
     
3.       Return the [default \``User-Agent`\` value](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
    

      
The *document \``Accept`\` header value* is\``text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8`\`.
   
#### 2.2.3. Statuses
   
A *status* is an integer in the range 0 to 999, inclusive.
   
Various edge cases in mapping HTTP/1’s `status-code` to this concept areworked on in [issue #1156](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
A *null body status* is a [status](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") that is 101, 103, 204, 205, or 304.
   
An *ok status* is a [status](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") in the range 200 to 299, inclusive.
   
A *range status* is a [status](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") that is 206 or 416.
   
A *redirect status* is a [status](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") that is 301, 302, 303, 307, or 308.
   
#### 2.2.4. Bodies
   
A *body* consists of:
   
    -      A *stream* (a `[ReadableStream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object). 
    
-      A *source* (null, a [byte sequence](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), a `[Blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object, or a `[FormData](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object), initially null. 
    
-      A *length* (null or an integer), initially null.
   
   

    
To *clone* a[body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") body, run these steps:
   

     1.       Let « out1, out2 » be the result of [teeing](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") body’s [stream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). 
     
2.       Set body’s [stream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to out1. 
     
3.       Return a [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") whose [stream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is out2 and other members are copied from body.
    

      

    
To get a [byte sequence](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") bytes*as a body*, return 
the [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") of theresult of [safely extracting](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")
bytes.
      
---
   

    
To *incrementally read* a [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") body, given analgorithm 
processBodyChunk, an algorithm processEndOfBody, an algorithmprocessBodyError, and 
an optional null, [parallel queue](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), or[global object](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle")
taskDestination (default null), run these steps.processBodyChunk must be an algorithm 
accepting a [byte sequence](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y'
key").processEndOfBody must be an algorithm accepting no arguments. processBodyErrormust 
be an algorithm accepting an exception.
   

     1.       If taskDestination is null, then set taskDestination to the result of [starting a new parallel queue](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/commits "You can also press the 'y' key"). 
     
2.       Let reader be the result of [getting a reader](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "Japanese") for  body’s [stream](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese").  
      
	This operation will not throw an exception. 
     
3.       Perform the [incrementally-read loop](ja "Simplified Chinese") given reader, taskDestination, processBodyChunk, processEndOfBody, and processBodyError.
    

      

    
To perform the *incrementally-read loop*, given a `[ReadableStreamDefaultReader](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
objectreader, [parallel queue](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") or [global object](#preface "Content Security Policy Level
3")taskDestination, algorithm processBodyChunk, algorithmprocessEndOfBody, and algorithm 
processBodyError:
   

     1.       Let readRequest be the following [read request](#http "Content Security Policy Level 3"):  
             [chunk steps](#terminology-headers "Content Security Policy Level 3"), given chunk                 

         1.           Let continueAlgorithm be null.     
         
2.           If chunk is not a `[Uint8Array](#requests "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object, then set     continueAlgorithm to this step: run processBodyError given a     `[TypeError](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3")`.     
         
3.           Otherwise:      
         

           1.             Let bytes be a [copy of](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3")        chunk.        
            
	Implementations are strongly encouraged to use an implementation strategy that        avoids this copy where possible.       
           
2.             Set continueAlgorithm to these steps:        
           

             1.               Run processBodyChunk given bytes.         
             
2.               Perform the [incrementally-read loop](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3") given reader,         taskDestination, processBodyChunk, processEndOfBody, and         processBodyError.        
            

          

         
-           [Queue a fetch task](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3") given continueAlgorithm and     taskDestination.    
        

       [close steps](#cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")                 

         1.           [Queue a fetch task](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") given processEndOfBody and   taskDestination.
        

       [error steps](#origin-header "Content Security Policy Level 3"), given e                 

         1.           [Queue a fetch task](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") to run processBodyError given e,   with taskDestination.
        

           
-       [Read a chunk](#http-responses "Content Security Policy Level 3") from reader given readRequest.
    

      

    
To *fully read* a [body](#cors-protocol-examples "Content Security Policy Level 3") body, 
given an algorithmprocessBody, an algorithm processBodyError, and an optional null,[parallel queue](#content-length-header "Content Security Policy Level 3"), or [global object](#content-type-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")
taskDestination (defaultnull), run these steps. processBody must be an algorithm 
accepting a[byte sequence](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3").
processBodyError must be an algorithm optionally accepting an[exception](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3").
   

     1.       If taskDestination is null, then set taskDestination to the result of [starting a new parallel queue](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
2.       Let successSteps given a [byte sequence](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3") bytes be to [queue a fetch task](#override-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") to run processBody given bytes, with taskDestination. 
     
3.       Let errorSteps optionally given an [exception](#http-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") exception be to [queue a fetch task](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") to run processBodyError given exception, with taskDestination. 
     
4.       Let reader be the result of [getting a reader](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") for body’s [stream](#cors-preflight-cache "Content Security Policy Level 3"). If that threw an exception, then run errorSteps with that exception and return. 
     
5.       [Read all bytes](#cors-check "Content Security Policy Level 3") from reader, given successSteps and errorSteps.
    

      
---
   
A *body with type* is a [tuple](#fetch-api "Content Security Policy Level 3") that consists of
a*body* (a [body](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3")) and a*type* 
(a [header value](#response-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") or null).
   
---
   

    
To *handle content codings* given codings and bytes, runthese steps:
   

     1.       If codings are not supported, then return bytes. 
     
2.       Return the result of decoding bytes with codings as explained in HTTP, if decoding does not result in an error, and failure otherwise. [[HTTP]](#http-header-layer-division "Content Security Policy Level 3")
    

      
#### 2.2.5. Requests
   
This section documents how requests work in detail. To get started, see[Setting up a request](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level 3").
   
The input to [fetch](#websocket-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") is a*request*.
   
A [request](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3") has an associated*method* 
(a[method](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3")). Unless stated otherwise it
is\``GET`\`.
   
This can be updated during redirects to \``GET`\` as described in[HTTP fetch](#acknowledgments "Content Security Policy Level 3").
   
A [request](#ipr "Content Security Policy Level 3") has an associated *URL*(a [URL](#index-defined-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3")).
   
Implementations are encouraged to make this a pointer to the first [URL](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") in[request](#normative "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [URL list](#informative "Content Security Policy Level 3"). It is provided as a distinct field solely forthe convenience of other standards hooking into Fetch.
   
A [request](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") has an associated*local-URLs-only 
flag*. Unless stated otherwise it isunset.
   
A [request](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") has an associated*header 
list* (a[header list](#biblio-sw "Fetch Metadata Request Headers")). Unless stated otherwise it is « ».
   
A [request](#biblio-mix "Mixed Content") has an associated*unsafe-request flag*. 
Unless stated otherwise itis unset.
   
The [unsafe-request flag](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css) is set by APIs such as[`fetch()`](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css "subtitle")  and `[XMLHttpRequest](h-entry "subtitle")` 
to ensure a [CORS-preflight fetch](h-entry "subtitle")is done based on the supplied [method](h-entry "subtitle") and [header list](h-entry "subtitle"). It doesnot free an API from outlawing [forbidden methods](h-entry "subtitle") and [forbidden request-headers](h-entry "subtitle").
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated*body* (null, a [byte sequence](h-entry "subtitle"), or a[body](h-entry "subtitle")). Unless stated otherwise it is null.
   
A [byte sequence](h-entry "subtitle") will be [safely extracted](h-entry "subtitle") into a[body](h-entry "subtitle") early on in [fetch](h-entry "subtitle"). As part of [HTTP fetch](h-entry "subtitle") it is possible forthis field to be set to null due to certain redirects.
   
---
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated*client* (null or an[environment settings object](h-entry "subtitle")).
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated*reserved client*(null or an [environment](h-entry "subtitle")). Unless stated otherwise it is null.
   
This is only used by [navigation requests](h-entry "subtitle") and worker requests, but not serviceworker requests.
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated*replaces client id*(a string). 
Unless stated otherwise it is the empty string.
   
This is only used by [navigation requests](h-entry "subtitle"). It is the [id](h-entry "subtitle")of the [target browsing context](h-entry "subtitle")’s [active document](h-entry "subtitle")’s[environment settings object](h-entry "subtitle").
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated*traversable for user prompts*, 
that is"`no-traversable`", "`client`", or a [traversable navigable](h-entry "subtitle").Unless stated otherwise it is
"`client`".
   

    
This is used to determine whether and where to show necessary UI for the request, 
such as authentication prompts or client certificate dialogs. 
         "`no-traversable`"       No UI is shown; usually the request fails with a [network error](h-entry "subtitle").      
"`client`"       This value will automatically be changed to either "`no-traversable`" or to a  [traversable navigable](h-entry "subtitle") derived from the request’s [client](h-entry "subtitle") during  [fetching](h-entry "subtitle"). This provides a convenient way for standards to not have to  explicitly set a request’s [traversable for user prompts](h-entry "subtitle").      
a [traversable navigable](h-entry "subtitle")       The UI shown will be associated 
with the browser interface elements that are displaying that  [traversable navigable](h-entry "subtitle").      
     
When displaying a user interface associated with a request in that request’s[traversable for user prompts](h-entry "subtitle"), the user agent should update the address bar todisplay something derived from the request’s [current URL](h-entry "subtitle") (and not, e.g., leaveit at its previous value, derived from the URL of the request’s initiator). Additionally, the useragent should avoid displaying content from the request’s initiator in the[traversable for user prompts](h-entry "subtitle"), especially in the case of cross-origin requests.Displaying a blank page behind such prompts is a good way to fulfill these requirements. Failing tofollow these guidelines can confuse users as to which origin is responsible for the prompt.
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated boolean*keepalive*. Unless stated 
otherwise it isfalse.
   
This can be used to allow the request to outlive the[environment settings object](h-entry "subtitle"), e.g., `navigator.sendBeacon()` 
and the HTML`img` element use this. Requests with this set to true are subject to additionalprocessing 
requirements.
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated*initiator type*, which is
null,"`audio`","`beacon`","`body`","`css`","`early-hints`","`embed`","`fetch`","`font`","`frame`","`iframe`","`image`","`img`","`input`","`link`","`object`","`ping`","`script`","`track`","`video`","`xmlhttprequest`", 
or"`other`". Unless stated otherwise it is null. [[RESOURCE-TIMING]](h-entry "subtitle")
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated *service-workers mode*, thatis 
"`all`" or "`none`". Unless stated otherwise it is "`all`".
   

    
This determines which service workers will receive a `[fetch](h-entry "subtitle")` event 
for this fetch. 
         "`all`"       Relevant service workers will get a `[fetch](h-entry "subtitle")` 
event for this fetch.       "`none`"       No service workers will get events for this 
fetch.           
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated*initiator*, which isthe empty
string,"`download`","`imageset`","`manifest`","`prefetch`","`prerender`", or"`xslt`". Unless stated otherwise 
it is the empty string.
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle")’s [initiator](h-entry "subtitle") is not particularly granular forthe time being as other specifications do not require it to be. It is primarily a specificationdevice to assist defining CSP and Mixed Content. It is not exposed to JavaScript. [[CSP]](h-entry "subtitle") [[MIX]](h-entry "subtitle")
   
A *destination type* is one of:the empty
string,"`audio`","`audioworklet`","`document`","`embed`","`font`","`frame`","`iframe`","`image`","`json`","`manifest`","`object`","`paintworklet`","`report`","`script`","`serviceworker`","`sharedworker`","`style`","`text`","`track`","`video`","`webidentity`","`worker`", 
or"`xslt`".
   
A [request](h-entry "subtitle") has an associated*destination*, which is[destination type](h-entry "subtitle"). Unless stated otherwise it is the empty string.
   
These are reflected on `[RequestDestination](h-entry "subtitle")` except for "`serviceworker`"and "`webidentity`" as fetches 
with those destinations skip service workers.
   
A [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [destination](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")
is*script-like* if it is "`audioworklet`","`paintworklet`", "`script`", "`serviceworker`","`sharedworker`", or "`worker`".
   
Algorithms that use [script-like](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") should also
consider"`xslt`" as that too can cause script execution. It is not included in the 
list as it isnot always relevant and might require different behavior.
   

         
The following table illustrates the relationship between a [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [initiator](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), [destination](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), CSP directives, and features. It is not exhaustive with respect to features. Features need to have the relevant values defined in their respective standards. 
    
|   |
||-------------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------|
| - | -                                                                 | -                                                                   | -                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
|   | ""                                                                | "`report`"                                                          | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | CSP, NEL reports.                                   |
|   | "`document`"                                                      | HTML’s navigate algorithm (top-level only).                       |
|   | "`frame`"                                                         | `child-src`                                                         | HTML’s `<frame>`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
|   | "`iframe`"                                                        | `child-src`                                                         | HTML’s `<iframe>`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
|   | ""                                                                | `connect-src`                                                       | `navigator.sendBeacon()`, `[EventSource](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`,   HTML’s `<a ping="">` and `<area ping="">`,   [`fetch()`](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), [`fetchLater()`](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), `[XMLHttpRequest](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`,   `[WebSocket](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`, `[WebTransport](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`, Cache API |
|   | "`object`"                                                        | `object-src`                                                        | HTML’s `<object>`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
|   | "`embed`"                                                         | `object-src`                                                        | HTML’s `<embed>`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
|   | "`audio`"                                                         | `media-src`                                                         | HTML’s `<audio>`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
|   | "`font`"                                                          | `font-src`                                                          | CSS' `@font-face`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
|   | "`image`"                                                         | `img-src`                                                           | HTML’s `<img src>`, `/favicon.ico` resource,   SVG’s `<image>`, CSS' `background-image`, CSS'   `cursor`, CSS' `list-style-image`, …                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
|   | "`audioworklet`"                                                  | `script-src`                                                        | `audioWorklet.addModule()`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
|   | "`paintworklet`"                                                  | `script-src`                                                        | `CSS.paintWorklet.addModule()`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
|   | "`script`"                                                        | `script-src`                                                        | HTML’s `<script>`, `importScripts()`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
|   | "`serviceworker`"                                                 | `child-src`, `script-src`, `worker-src`                             | `navigator.serviceWorker.register()`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
|   | "`sharedworker`"                                                  | `child-src`, `script-src`, `worker-src`                             | `SharedWorker`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
|   | "`webidentity`"                                                   | `connect-src`                                                       | `Federated Credential Management requests`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
|   | "`worker`"                                                        | `child-src`, `script-src`, `worker-src`                             | `Worker`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
|   | "`json`"                                                          | `connect-src`                                                       | `import "..." with { type: "json" }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
|   | "`style`"                                                         | `style-src`                                                         | HTML’s `<link rel=stylesheet>`, CSS' `@import`, `import "..." with { type: "css" }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
|   | "`text`"                                                          | `connect-src`                                                       | `import "..." with { type: "text" }`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
|   | "`track`"                                                         | `media-src`                                                         | HTML’s `<track>`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
|   | "`video`"                                                         | `media-src`                                                         | HTML’s `<video>` element                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
|   | "`download`"                                                      | ""                                                                  | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | HTML’s `download=""`, "Save Link As…" UI        |
|   | "`imageset`"                                                      | "`image`"                                                           | `img-src`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | HTML’s `<img srcset>` and `<picture>` |

|   | "`prerender`"                                                     | HTML’s `<link rel=prerender>`                               |
