-           Let serializedFullLength be fullLength,     [serialized](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") and [isomorphic encoded](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     
         
-           Let type be blob’s `[type](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.     
         
-           If request’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")      [does not contain](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") \``Range`\`:      
         

           1.             Let bodyWithType be the result of [safely extracting](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")       blob.       
           
2.             Set response’s [status message](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to \``OK`\`.       
           
3.             Set response’s [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to bodyWithType’s       [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       
           
4.             Set response’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to «       (\``Content-Length`\`, serializedFullLength),       (\``Content-Type`\`, type) ».      
          

         
-           Otherwise:      
         

           1.             Set response’s [range-requested flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       
           
2.             Let rangeHeader be the result of [getting](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")       \``Range`\` from request’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       
           
3.             Let rangeValue be the result of [parsing a single range header value](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")       given rangeHeader and true.       
           
4.             If rangeValue is failure, then return a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       
           
5.             Let (rangeStart, rangeEnd) be rangeValue.       
           
6.             If rangeStart is null:        
           

             1.               Set rangeStart to fullLength − rangeEnd.         
             
2.               Set rangeEnd to rangeStart + rangeEnd − 1.        
            

           
-             Otherwise:        
           

             1.               If rangeStart is greater than or equal to fullLength, then         return a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").         
             
2.               If rangeEnd is null or rangeEnd is greater than or equal to         fullLength, then set rangeEnd to fullLength − 1.        
            

           
-             Let slicedBlob be the result of invoking [slice blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") given        blob, rangeStart, rangeEnd + 1, and type.        
            
	A range header denotes an inclusive byte range, while the [slice blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")        algorithm input range does not. To use the [slice blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") algorithm, we have to increment        rangeEnd.       
           
-             Let slicedBodyWithType be the result of       [safely extracting](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") slicedBlob.       
           
-             Set response’s [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to slicedBodyWithType’s       [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       
           
-             Let serializedSlicedLength be slicedBlob’s `[size](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`,       [serialized](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") and [isomorphic encoded](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       
           
-             Let contentRange be the result of invoking [build a content range](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")       given rangeStart, rangeEnd, and fullLength.       
           
-             Set response’s [status](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to 206.       
           
-             Set response’s [status message](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to       \``Partial Content`\`.       
           
-             Set response’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to «       (\``Content-Length`\`, serializedSlicedLength),       (\``Content-Type`\`, type), (\``Content-Range`\`,       contentRange) ».      
          

         
-           Return response.    
        

       "`data`"                 

         1.           Let dataURLStruct be the result of running the     [`data:` URL processor](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") on request’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     
         
2.           If dataURLStruct is failure, then return a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     
         
3.           Let mimeType be dataURLStruct’s     [MIME type](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), [serialized](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     
         
4.           Return a new [response](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") whose [status message](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle") is     \``OK`\`, [header list](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle") is « (\``Content-Type`\`,     mimeType) », and [body](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key") is dataURLStruct’s     [body](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues "You can also press the 'y' key") [as a body](https://whatwg.org/chat "You can also press the 'y' key").    
        

       "`file`"                  
For now, unfortunate as it is, `file:` [URLs](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key") are left as an exercise    for the reader.    
        
When in doubt, return a [network error](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese").   
       [HTTP(S) scheme](https://triple-underscore.github.io/Fetch-ja.html "Japanese")           
       
Return the result of running [HTTP fetch](ja "Simplified Chinese") given fetchParams. 
 
           
-       
Return a [network error](https://ko.htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Korean").
    

      

    
To *determine the environment*, given a [request](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")request:
   

     1.       If request’s [reserved client](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null, then return request’s [reserved client](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
2.       If request’s [client](#url "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null, then return request’s [client](#methods "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
3.       Return null.
    

      
### 4.4. HTTP fetch
   

    
To *HTTP fetch*, given a [fetch params](#responses "Content Security Policy Level 3")fetchParams 
and an optional boolean makeCORSPreflight (default false), runthese steps:
   

     1.       Let request be fetchParams’s [request](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
2.       Let response and internalResponse be null. 
     
3.       If request’s [service-workers mode](#connections "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`all`", then:  
     

       1.         Let requestForServiceWorker be a [clone](#http-cache-partitions "Content Security Policy Level 3") of   request.   
       
2.         If requestForServiceWorker’s [body](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null, then:    
       

         1.           Let transformStream be a new `[TransformStream](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3")`.     
         
2.           Let transformAlgorithm given chunk be these steps:      
         

           1.             If fetchParams is [canceled](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then abort these       steps.       
           
2.             If chunk is not a `[Uint8Array](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object, then       [terminate](#cookie-infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3") fetchParams’s       [controller](#origin-header "Content Security Policy Level 3").       
           
3.             Otherwise, [enqueue](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") chunk in       transformStream. The user agent may split the chunk into       [implementation-defined](#http-requests "Content Security Policy Level 3") practical sizes and [enqueue](#http-new-header-syntax "Content Security Policy Level 3") each of       them. The user agent also may concatenate the chunks into an [implementation-defined](#cors-protocol-and-credentials "Content Security Policy Level 3")       practical size and [enqueue](#cors-protocol-exceptions "Content Security Policy Level 3") it.      
          

         
-           [Set up](#content-length-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") transformStream with     [*transformAlgorithm*](#content-type-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") set to     transformAlgorithm.     
         
-           Set requestForServiceWorker’s [body](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [stream](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") to     the result of requestForServiceWorker’s [body](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [stream](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")     [piped through](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") transformStream.    
        

       
-         Let serviceWorkerStartTime be the [coarsened shared current time](#main-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")   given fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3").   
       
-         Let fetchResponse be the result of invoking [handle fetch](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") for   requestForServiceWorker, with fetchParams’s   [controller](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") and fetchParams’s   [cross-origin isolated capability](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3"). [[HTML]](#cors-preflight-cache "Content Security Policy Level 3") [[SW]](#cors-check "Content Security Policy Level 3")   
       
-         If fetchResponse is a [response](#tao-check "Content Security Policy Level 3"):    
       

         1.           Set response to fetchResponse.     
         
2.           Set fetchParams’s [timing info](#deferred-fetch-quota "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s     [final service worker start time](#fetch-api "Content Security Policy Level 3") to     serviceWorkerStartTime.     
         
3.           Set fetchParams’s [timing info](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s     [service worker timing info](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3") to response’s     [service worker timing info](#response-class "Content Security Policy Level 3").     
         
4.           If request’s [body](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null, then     [cancel](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3") request’s [body](#background-reading "Content Security Policy Level 3") with undefined.     
         
5.           Set internalResponse to response, if response is not a     [filtered response](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3"); otherwise to response’s     [internal response](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3").     
         
6.           Run the [WebDriver BiDi response started](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level 3") steps with     request and response.     
         
7.           If one of the following is true      
                     8.             response’s [type](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`error`"       
           
9.             request’s [mode](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`same-origin`" and       response’s [type](#acknowledgments "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`cors`"       
           
10.             request’s [mode](#ipr "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not "`no-cors`" and       response’s [type](#index "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`opaque`"       
           
11. request’s [redirect mode](#index-defined-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not "`manual`" and       response’s [type](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`opaqueredirect`"                  
12. request’s [redirect mode](#informative "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not "`follow`" and       response’s [URL list](#idl-index "Content Security Policy Level 3") has more than one item                
          then return a [network error](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3").    
        

       
-         Otherwise, if fetchResponse is a [service worker timing info](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3"),   then set fetchParams’s [timing info](#biblio-fetch-metadata "Fetch Metadata Request Headers")’s   [service worker timing info](#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly") to fetchResponse.  
      

     
-       
If response is null, then:  
     

       1.         If makeCORSPreflight is true and one of these conditions is true:    
                 2.           There is no [method cache entry match](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css) for request’s     [method](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css "subtitle") using request, and either request’s     [method](h-entry "subtitle") is not a [CORS-safelisted method](h-entry "subtitle") or request’s     [use-CORS-preflight flag](h-entry "subtitle") is set.     
         
3. There is at least one [item](h-entry "subtitle") in the [CORS-unsafe request-header names](h-entry "subtitle")     with request’s [header list](h-entry "subtitle") for which there is no     [header-name cache entry match](h-entry "subtitle") using request.            
        
Then:    
       

         1.           Let preflightResponse be the result of running [CORS-preflight fetch](h-entry "subtitle")     given request.     
         
2.           If preflightResponse is a [network error](h-entry "subtitle"), then return     preflightResponse.    
        

        
This step checks the [CORS-preflight cache](h-entry "subtitle") and if there is no suitable entry    it performs a [CORS-preflight fetch](h-entry "subtitle") which, if successful, populates the cache. The purpose    of the [CORS-preflight fetch](h-entry "subtitle") is to ensure the [fetched](h-entry "subtitle") resource is    familiar with the [CORS protocol](h-entry "subtitle"). The cache is there to minimize the number of    [CORS-preflight fetches](h-entry "subtitle").   
       
-         
If request’s [redirect mode](h-entry "subtitle") is "`follow`", then set    request’s 
[service-workers mode](h-entry "subtitle") to "`none`".    
        
Redirects coming from the network (as opposed to from a service worker) are not to 
   be exposed to a service worker.   
       
-         
Set response and internalResponse to the result of running   [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](h-entry "subtitle") given
fetchParams.   
       
-         
If request’s [response tainting](h-entry "subtitle") is "`cors`" and a    [CORS check](h-entry "subtitle") for
request and response returns failure, then return a    [network error](h-entry "subtitle").    
        
As the [CORS check](h-entry "subtitle") is not to be applied to [responses](h-entry "subtitle") whose    [status](h-entry "subtitle") is 304 or 407, or [responses](h-entry "subtitle") from a service worker for    that matter, it is applied here.   
       
-         
If the [TAO check](h-entry "subtitle") for request and response returns failure, 
  then set request’s [timing allow failed flag](h-entry "subtitle").  
      

     
-       
If either request’s [response tainting](h-entry "subtitle") or response’s  [type](h-entry "subtitle") is
"`opaque`", and the  [cross-origin resource policy check](h-entry "subtitle") with request’s 
[origin](h-entry "subtitle"),  request’s [client](h-entry "subtitle"), request’s 
 [destination](h-entry "subtitle"), and internalResponse returns **blocked**, then 
 return a [network error](h-entry "subtitle").  
      
The [cross-origin resource policy check](h-entry "subtitle") runs for responses coming from the  network and responses coming from the service worker. This is different from the  [CORS check](h-entry "subtitle"), as
request’s [client](h-entry "subtitle") and the service worker can  have different embedder policies. 
     
-       
If internalResponse’s [status](h-entry "subtitle") is a [redirect status](h-entry "subtitle"):  
     

       1.         If request is a [navigation request](h-entry "subtitle"), then   [append to a request’s navigation timing allow values list](h-entry "subtitle") given request and   internalResponse.   
       
2.         If internalResponse’s [status](h-entry "subtitle") is not 303, request’s    [body](h-entry "subtitle") is non-null, and the [connection](h-entry "subtitle") uses HTTP/2, then user agents    may, and are even encouraged to, transmit an `RST_STREAM` frame.    
        
	303 is excluded as certain communities ascribe special status to it.   
       
3.         Switch on request’s    [redirect mode](h-entry "subtitle"):    
                 "`error`"                       

           1.             Set response to a [network error](h-entry "subtitle").
          

         "`manual`"                       

           1.             If request’s [mode](h-entry "subtitle") is "`navigate`", then set       fetchParams’s [controller](h-entry "subtitle")’s       [next manual redirect steps](h-entry "subtitle") to run [HTTP-redirect fetch](h-entry "subtitle")       given fetchParams and response.       
           
2.             Otherwise, set response to an [opaque-redirect filtered response](h-entry "subtitle")       whose [internal response](h-entry "subtitle") is internalResponse.      
          

         "`follow`"                       

           1. Run the [WebDriver BiDi response completed](h-entry "subtitle") steps with request and       response.                  
2.             Set response to the result of running [HTTP-redirect fetch](h-entry "subtitle") given       fetchParams and response.      
          

              

     
-       
Return response. Typically internalResponse’s [body](h-entry "subtitle")’s [stream](h-entry "subtitle") is still being enqueued to after returning.
    

      
### 4.5. HTTP-redirect fetch
   

    
To *HTTP-redirect fetch*, given a[fetch params](h-entry "subtitle") fetchParams and 
a [response](h-entry "subtitle") response,run these steps:
   

     1.       Let request be fetchParams’s [request](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
2.       Let internalResponse be response, if response is not a [filtered response](h-entry "subtitle"); otherwise response’s [internal response](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
3.       Let locationURL be internalResponse’s [location URL](h-entry "subtitle") given request’s [current URL](h-entry "subtitle")’s [fragment](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
4.       If locationURL is null, then return response. 
     
5.       If locationURL is failure, then return a [network error](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
6.       If locationURL’s [scheme](h-entry "subtitle") is not an [HTTP(S) scheme](h-entry "subtitle"), then return a [network error](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
7.       If request’s [redirect count](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is 20, then return a [network error](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"). 
     
8.       Increase request’s [redirect count](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") by 1. 
     
9.       If request’s [mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`cors`", locationURL [includes credentials](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), and request’s [origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is not [same origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") with locationURL’s [origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then return a [network error](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"). 
     
10.       If request’s [response tainting](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`cors`" and  locationURL [includes credentials](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then return a [network error](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").  
      
	This catches a cross-origin resource redirecting to a same-origin URL. 
     
11.       If internalResponse’s [status](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is not 303, request’s [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is non-null, and request’s [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [source](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is null, then return a [network error](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"). 
     
12.       If one of the following is true  
             13.         internalResponse’s [status](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is 301 or 302 and   request’s [method](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is \``POST`\`   
       
14.         internalResponse’s [status](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is 303 and request’s   [method](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is not \``GET`\` or \``HEAD`\`  
      
      
then:  
     

       1.         Set request’s [method](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to \``GET`\` and   request’s [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to null.   
       
2.         [For each](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") headerName of [request-body-header name](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"),   [delete](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") headerName from request’s   [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").  
      

     
-       
If request’s [current URL](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is not  [same origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") with
locationURL’s [origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then  [for each](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")
headerName of [CORS non-wildcard request-header name](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"),  [delete](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")
headerName from request’s  [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").  
      
I.e., the moment another origin is seen after the initial request, the  \``Authorization`\` header 
is removed. 
     
-       
If request’s [body](# "subtitle") is non-null, then set request’s  [body](# "subtitle") to the [body](# "subtitle") of the result of  [safely extracting](# "subtitle")
request’s [body](# "subtitle")’s  [source](# "subtitle").  
      
request’s [body](# "subtitle")’s [source](# "subtitle")’s nullity has  already been checked. 
     
-       
Let timingInfo be fetchParams’s [timing info](# "subtitle"). 
     
-       
Set timingInfo’s [redirect end time](# "subtitle") and [post-redirect start time](# "subtitle") to the [coarsened shared current time](# "subtitle") given
fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](# "subtitle"). 
     
-       
If timingInfo’s [redirect start time](# "subtitle") is 0, then set timingInfo’s 
[redirect start time](# "subtitle") to timingInfo’s [start time](# "subtitle"). 
     
-       
[Append](# "subtitle") locationURL to request’s [URL list](# "subtitle"). 
     
-       
Invoke [set request’s referrer policy on redirect](# "subtitle") on request and 
internalResponse. [[REFERRER]](# "subtitle") 
     
-       
Let recursive be true. 
     
-       
If request’s [redirect mode](# "subtitle") is "`manual`", then:  
     

       1.         [Assert](# "subtitle"): request’s [mode](# "subtitle") is   "`navigate`".   
       
2.         Set recursive to false.  
      

     
-       
Return the result of running [main fetch](# "subtitle") given fetchParams and  recursive. 
 
      
This has to invoke [main fetch](# "subtitle") to get request’s  [response tainting](# "subtitle") correct.
    

      
### 4.6. HTTP-network-or-cache fetch
   

    
To *HTTP-network-or-cache fetch*, given a[fetch params](# "subtitle") fetchParams, 
an optional booleanisAuthenticationFetch (default false), and an optional
booleanisNewConnectionFetch (default false), run these steps:
    
Some implementations might support caching of partial content, as per*HTTP Caching*. 
However, this is not widely supported by browser caches.[[HTTP-CACHING]](# "subtitle")
   

     1.       Let request be fetchParams’s [request](# "subtitle"). 
     
2.       Let httpFetchParams be null. 
     
3.       Let httpRequest be null. 
     
4.       Let response be null. 
     
5.       Let storedResponse be null. 
     
6.       Let httpCache be null. 
     
7.       Let the revalidatingFlag be unset. 
     
8.       Run these steps, but [abort when](# "subtitle") fetchParams is  [canceled](# "subtitle"):  
     

       1.         If request’s [traversable for user prompts](# "subtitle") is    "`no-traversable`" and request’s [redirect mode](# "subtitle") is    "`error`", then set httpFetchParams to fetchParams and    httpRequest to request.   
       
2.         Otherwise:    
       

         1.           Set httpRequest to a [clone](# "subtitle") of request.      
          
	Implementations are encouraged to avoid teeing request’s      [body](# "subtitle")’s [stream](# "subtitle") when request’s      [body](# "subtitle")’s [source](# "subtitle") is null as only a single body is needed in      that case. E.g., when request’s [body](# "subtitle")’s [source](# "subtitle")      is null, redirects and authentication will end up failing the fetch.     
         
2.           Set httpFetchParams to a copy of fetchParams.     
         
3.           Set httpFetchParams’s [request](# "subtitle") to     httpRequest.    
        

        If user prompts or redirects are possible, then the user agent might need to    re-send the request with a new set of headers after the user answers the prompt or the redirect    location is determined. At that time, the original request body might have been partially sent    already, so we need to clone the request (including the body) beforehand so that we have a    spare copy available.   
       
-         Let includeCredentials be true if one of    
                 - request’s [credentials mode](# "subtitle") is     "`include`"              
- request’s  [credentials mode](# "subtitle") is     "`same-origin`" and request’s     [response tainting](# "subtitle") is "`basic`"            
        is true; otherwise false.   
       
-         If [Cross-Origin-Embedder-Policy allows credentials](# "subtitle") with request returns   false, then set includeCredentials to false.   
       
-         Let contentLength be httpRequest’s [body](# "subtitle")’s   [length](# "subtitle"), if httpRequest’s [body](# "subtitle") is non-null;   otherwise null.   
       
-         Let contentLengthHeaderValue be null.   
       
-         If httpRequest’s [body](# "subtitle") is null and httpRequest’s   [method](# "subtitle") is \``POST`\` or \``PUT`\`, then set   contentLengthHeaderValue to \``0`\`.   
       
-         If contentLength is non-null, then set  contentLengthHeaderValue to   contentLength, [serialized](# "subtitle") and   [isomorphic encoded](# "subtitle").   
       
-         If contentLengthHeaderValue is non-null, then [append](# "subtitle")   (\``Content-Length`\`, contentLengthHeaderValue) to httpRequest’s   [header list](# "subtitle").   
       
-         If contentLength is non-null and httpRequest’s    [keepalive](# "subtitle") is true, then:    
       

         1.           Let inflightKeepaliveBytes be 0.     
         
2.           Let group be httpRequest’s [client](# "subtitle")’s     [fetch group](# "subtitle").     
         
3.           Let inflightRecords be the set of [fetch records](# "subtitle") in     group whose [request](# "subtitle")’s [keepalive](# "subtitle") is true     and [done flag](# "subtitle") is unset.     
         
4.           [For each](# "subtitle") fetchRecord of inflightRecords:      
         

           1.             Let inflightRequest be fetchRecord’s       [request](# "subtitle").       
           
2.             Increment inflightKeepaliveBytes by inflightRequest’s       [body](# "subtitle")’s [length](# "subtitle").      
          

         
-           If the sum of contentLength and inflightKeepaliveBytes is greater     than 64 kibibytes, then return a [network error](# "subtitle").    
        

        The above limit ensures that requests that are allowed to outlive the    [environment settings object](# "subtitle") and contain a body, have a bounded size and are not allowed    to stay alive indefinitely.   
       
-         If httpRequest’s [referrer](# "subtitle") is a [URL](# "subtitle"), then:    
       

         1.           Let referrerValue be httpRequest’s [referrer](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"),     [serialized](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") and [isomorphic encoded](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").     
         
2.           [Append](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") (\``Referer`\`, referrerValue) to     httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").    
        

       
-         [Append a request \``Origin`\` header](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") for httpRequest.   
       
-         [Append the Fetch metadata headers for httpRequest](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").   [[FETCH-METADATA]](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")   
       
-         If httpRequest’s [initiator](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") is "`prefetch`", then   [set a structured field value](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") given (\`[`Sec-Purpose`](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")\`,   the [token](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") `prefetch`) in   httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").   
       
-         If httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") [does not contain](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") \``User-Agent`\`, then user agents should:    
       

         1.           Let userAgent be httpRequest’s [client](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s     [environment default \``User-Agent`\` value](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").     
         
2.           [Append](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") (\``User-Agent`\`, userAgent) to     httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").    
        

       
-         If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") is "`default`" and   httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") [contains](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")   \``If-Modified-Since`\`,   \``If-None-Match`\`,   \``If-Unmodified-Since`\`,   \``If-Match`\`, or   \``If-Range`\`, then set httpRequest’s   [cache mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") to "`no-store`".   
       
-         If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") is "`no-cache`",   httpRequest’s [prevent no-cache cache-control header modification flag](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")   is unset, and httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")   [does not contain](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") \``Cache-Control`\`, then   [append](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") (\``Cache-Control`\`, \``max-age=0`\`) to   httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle").   
       
-         If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") is "`no-store`" or    "`reload`", then:    
       

         1.           If httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")     [does not contain](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") \``Pragma`\`, then     [append](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") (\``Pragma`\`, \``no-cache`\`) to     httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle").     
         
2.           If httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")     [does not contain](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") \``Cache-Control`\`, then     [append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") (\``Cache-Control`\`, \``no-cache`\`) to     httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    
        

       
-         If httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [contains](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")    \``Range`\`, then [append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") (\``Accept-Encoding`\`,    \``identity`\`) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    
        

         This avoids a failure when [handling content codings](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") with     a part of an encoded [response](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     
         
	Additionally,     [many servers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")     mistakenly ignore \``Range`\` headers if a non-identity encoding is accepted.    
               
-         Modify httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") per HTTP. Do not    [append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a given [header](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") if httpRequest’s    [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [contains](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") that [header](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s    [name](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    
        
	It would be great if we could make this more normative somehow. At this point    [headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") such as    \``Accept-Encoding`\`,    \``Connection`\`,    \``DNT`\`, and    \``Host`\`,    are to be [appended](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") if necessary.    
        
	\``Accept`\`,    \``Accept-Charset`\`, and    \``Accept-Language`\` must not be included at this point.    
        
	\``Accept`\` and \``Accept-Language`\` are already included    (unless [`fetch()`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is used, which does not include the latter by    default), and \``Accept-Charset`\` is a waste of bytes. See    [HTTP header layer division](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for more details.   
       
-         If includeCredentials is true, then:    
       

         1.           [Append a request \``Cookie`\` header](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for httpRequest.     
         
2.           If httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")      [does not contain](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") \``Authorization`\`, then:      
         

           1.             Let authorizationValue be null.       
           
2.             If there’s an [authentication entry](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for httpRequest and either       httpRequest’s [use-URL-credentials flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is unset or       httpRequest’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") does not [include credentials](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),       then set authorizationValue to [authentication entry](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       
           
3.             Otherwise, if httpRequest’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") does       [include credentials](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") and isAuthenticationFetch is true, set       authorizationValue to httpRequest’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),       converted to an \``Authorization`\` value.       
           
4.             If authorizationValue is non-null, then [append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")       (\``Authorization`\`, authorizationValue) to httpRequest’s       [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").      
          

        

       
-         If there’s a [proxy-authentication entry](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), use it as appropriate.    
        
	This intentionally does not depend on httpRequest’s    [credentials mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   
       
-         Run the [WebDriver BiDi before request sent](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") steps with request.   
       
-         Set httpCache to the result of [determining the HTTP cache partition](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),   given httpRequest.   
       
-         If httpCache is null, then set httpRequest’s   [cache mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to "`no-store`".   
       
-         If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is neither "`no-store`"    nor "`reload`", then:    
       

         1.           Set storedResponse to the result of selecting a response from the      httpCache, possibly needing validation, as per the      "[Constructing Responses from Caches](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")" chapter of *HTTP Caching*, if any.      [[HTTP-CACHING]](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")      
          
	As mandated by HTTP, this still takes the \``Vary`\`      [header](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") into account.     
         
2.           If storedResponse is non-null, then:      
         

           1.             If [cache mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is "`default`", storedResponse        is a [stale-while-revalidate response](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), and httpRequest’s        [client](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is non-null, then:        
           

             1.               Set response to storedResponse.         
             
2.               Set response’s [cache state](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle") to "`local`".         
             
3.               Let revalidateRequest be a [clone](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key") of         request.         
             
4.               Set revalidateRequest’s [cache mode](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues "You can also press the 'y' key") set to         "`no-cache`".         
             
5.               Set revalidateRequest’s         [prevent no-cache cache-control header modification flag](/commit-snapshots/394d20d144ed1401c2c0e02c35bc3608cb2a2269/ "You can also press the 'y' key").         
             
6.               Set revalidateRequest’s [service-workers mode](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese") set to         "`none`".         
             
7.               [In parallel](https://triple-underscore.github.io/Fetch-ja.html "Japanese"), run [main fetch](https://htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Simplified Chinese") given a new [fetch params](https://ko.htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Korean") whose          [request](ko "Content Security Policy Level 3") is revalidateRequest.          
              
	This fetch is only meant to update the state of httpCache          and the response will be unused until another cache access. The stale response will be used          as the response to current request. This fetch is issued in the context of a client so if          it goes away the request will be terminated.        
            

           
-             Otherwise:        
           

             1.               If storedResponse is a [stale response](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then set the         revalidatingFlag.         
             
2.               If the revalidatingFlag is set and httpRequest’s          [cache mode](#http "Content Security Policy Level 3") is neither "`force-cache`" nor          "`only-if-cached`", then:          
             

               1.                 If storedResponse’s [header list](#statuses "Content Security Policy Level 3")           [contains](#bodies "Content Security Policy Level 3") \``ETag`\`, then           [append](#requests "Content Security Policy Level 3") (\``If-None-Match`\`, \``ETag`\`'s           [value](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3")) to httpRequest’s [header list](#authentication-entries "Content Security Policy Level 3").           
               
2.                 If storedResponse’s [header list](#resolving-domains "Content Security Policy Level 3")           [contains](#connections "Content Security Policy Level 3") \``Last-Modified`\`, then           [append](#network-partition-keys "Content Security Policy Level 3") (\``If-Modified-Since`\`,           \``Last-Modified`\`'s [value](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3")) to httpRequest’s           [header list](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-mime-type? "Content Security Policy Level 3").          
              

              See also the "[Sending a Validation Request](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3")" chapter of          *HTTP Caching*. [[HTTP-CACHING]](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3")         
             
-               Otherwise, set response to storedResponse and set         response’s [cache state](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") to "`local`".        
            

          

        

      

     
-       
[If aborted](#origin-header "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then return the [appropriate network error](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") for
fetchParams. 
     
-       
If response is not null, then run the [WebDriver BiDi response started](#general "Content Security Policy Level 3") steps with
request and response. 
     
-       
If response is null, then:  
     

       1.         If httpRequest’s [cache mode](#http-new-header-syntax "Content Security Policy Level 3") is   "`only-if-cached`", then return a [network error](#cors-protocol-examples "Content Security Policy Level 3").   
       
2.         Let forwardResponse be the result of running [HTTP-network fetch](#cors-protocol-exceptions "Content Security Policy Level 3") given   httpFetchParams, includeCredentials, and isNewConnectionFetch.   
       
3.         If httpRequest’s [method](#content-type-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") is [unsafe](#x-content-type-options-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") and   forwardResponse’s [status](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3") is in the range 200 to 399, inclusive,   invalidate appropriate stored responses in httpCache, as per the   "[Invalidating Stored Responses](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")" chapter of *HTTP Caching*, and set   storedResponse to null. [[HTTP-CACHING]](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")   
       
4.         If the revalidatingFlag is set and forwardResponse’s    [status](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3") is 304, then:    
       

         1.           Update storedResponse’s [header list](#override-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") using      forwardResponse’s [header list](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3"), as per the      "[Freshening Stored Responses upon Validation](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")" chapter of *HTTP Caching*.      [[HTTP-CACHING]](#http-network-or-cache-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")      
          
	This updates the stored response in cache as well.     
         
2.           Set response to storedResponse.     
         
3.           Set response’s [cache state](#cors-preflight-cache "Content Security Policy Level 3") to "`validated`".    
        

       
-         If response is null, then:    
       

         1.           Set response to forwardResponse.     
         
2.           Store httpRequest and forwardResponse in httpCache, as per      the "[Storing Responses in Caches](#deferred-fetch-quota "Content Security Policy Level 3")" chapter of *HTTP Caching*.      [[HTTP-CACHING]](#fetch-api "Content Security Policy Level 3")      
          
	If forwardResponse is a [network error](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3"), this effectively caches      the network error, which is sometimes known as "negative caching".      
          
	The associated [body info](#request-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") is stored in the cache      alongside the response.    
        

      

     
-       
Set response’s [URL list](#fetch-method "Content Security Policy Level 3") to a [clone](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3") of
httpRequest’s [URL list](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
-       
If httpRequest’s [header list](#http-header-layer-division "Content Security Policy Level 3") [contains](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3")
\``Range`\`, then set response’s [range-requested flag](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
-       
Set response’s [request-includes-credentials](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level 3") to
includeCredentials. 
     
-       
If response’s [status](#fetch-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3") is 401, httpRequest’s 
 [response tainting](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not "`cors`", 
includeCredentials is  true, and request’s [traversable for user prompts](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3") is a  [traversable navigable](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3"):  
     

       1.         Needs testing: multiple \``WWW-Authenticate`\` headers, missing,   parsing issues.   
       
2.         If request’s [body](#index "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null, then:    
       

         1.           If request’s [body](#index-defined-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [source](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") is null,     then return a [network error](#normative "Content Security Policy Level 3").     
         
2.           Set request’s [body](#informative "Content Security Policy Level 3") to the [body](#idl-index "Content Security Policy Level 3")     of the result of [safely extracting](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") request’s     [body](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [source](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3").    
        

       
-         If request’s [use-URL-credentials flag](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") is unset or    isAuthenticationFetch is true, then:    
       

         1.           If fetchParams is [canceled](#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly"), then return the     [appropriate network error](#biblio-upgrade-insecure-requests "Mixed Content") for fetchParams.     
         
2.           Let username and password be the result of prompting the end user     for a username and password, respectively, in request’s     [traversable for user prompts](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css).     
         
3.           [Set the username](h-entry "subtitle") given request’s [current URL](h-entry "subtitle") and     username.     
         
4.           [Set the password](h-entry "subtitle") given request’s [current URL](h-entry "subtitle") and     password.    
        

       
-         Set response to the result of running [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](h-entry "subtitle") given   fetchParams and true.  
      

     
-       
If response’s [status](h-entry "subtitle") is 407, then:  
     

       1.         If request’s [traversable for user prompts](h-entry "subtitle") is   "`no-traversable`", then return a [network error](h-entry "subtitle").   
       
2.         Needs testing: multiple \``Proxy-Authenticate`\` headers, missing,   parsing issues.   
       
3.         If fetchParams is [canceled](h-entry "subtitle"), then return the   [appropriate network error](h-entry "subtitle") for fetchParams.   
       
4.         Prompt the end user as appropriate in request’s    [traversable for user prompts](h-entry "subtitle") and store the result as a    [proxy-authentication entry](h-entry "subtitle"). [[HTTP]](h-entry "subtitle")    
        
	Remaining details surrounding proxy authentication are defined by HTTP.   
       
5.         Set response to the result of running [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](h-entry "subtitle") given   fetchParams.  
      

     
-       
If all of the following are true  
      
       -         response’s [status](h-entry "subtitle") is 421   
       
-         isNewConnectionFetch is false   
       
-         request’s [body](h-entry "subtitle") is null, or request’s   [body](h-entry "subtitle") is non-null and request’s [body](h-entry "subtitle")’s   [source](h-entry "subtitle") is non-null  
      
      
then:  
     

       1.         If fetchParams is [canceled](h-entry "subtitle"), then return the   [appropriate network error](h-entry "subtitle") for fetchParams.   
       
2.         Set response to the result of running [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](h-entry "subtitle") given   fetchParams, isAuthenticationFetch, and true.  
      

     
-       
If isAuthenticationFetch is true, then create an [authentication entry](h-entry "subtitle") for
request and the given realm. 
     
-       
Return response. Typically response’s [body](h-entry "subtitle")’s [stream](h-entry "subtitle") is still being enqueued to after returning.
    

      
### 4.7. HTTP-network fetch
   

    
To *HTTP-network fetch*, given a [fetch params](h-entry "subtitle")fetchParams, an 
optional boolean includeCredentials (default false), and anoptional boolean forceNewConnection 
(default false), run these steps:
   

     1.       Let request be fetchParams’s [request](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
2.       If request’s [client](h-entry "subtitle") [is offline](h-entry "subtitle"), then return a [network error](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
3.       Let response be null. 
     
4.       Let timingInfo be fetchParams’s [timing info](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
5.       Let networkPartitionKey be the result of [determining the network partition key](h-entry "subtitle") given request. 
     
6.       Let newConnection be "`yes`" if forceNewConnection is true; otherwise "`no`". 
     
7.       Switch on request’s [mode](h-entry "subtitle"):  
             "`websocket`"                  Let connection be the result of   [obtaining a WebSocket connection](h-entry "subtitle"), given   request’s [current URL](h-entry "subtitle").   
       "`webtransport`"                  Let connection be the result of [obtaining a WebTransport connection](h-entry "subtitle"),   given networkPartitionKey and request.   
       Otherwise                  Let connection be the result of   [obtaining a connection](h-entry "subtitle"), given networkPartitionKey,   request’s [current URL](h-entry "subtitle"), includeCredentials, and   newConnection.  
           
8.       Run these steps, but [abort when](h-entry "subtitle") fetchParams is  [canceled](h-entry "subtitle"):  
     

       1.         If connection is failure, then return a [network error](h-entry "subtitle").   
       
2.         Set timingInfo’s [final connection timing info](h-entry "subtitle") to   the result of calling [clamp and coarsen connection timing info](h-entry "subtitle") with   connection’s [timing info](h-entry "subtitle"), timingInfo’s   [post-redirect start time](h-entry "subtitle"), and fetchParams’s   [cross-origin isolated capability](h-entry "subtitle").   
       
3.         If connection is an HTTP/1.x connection, request’s   [body](h-entry "subtitle") is non-null, and request’s [body](h-entry "subtitle")’s   [source](h-entry "subtitle") is null, then return a [network error](h-entry "subtitle").   
       
4. Set timingInfo’s [final network-request start time](h-entry "subtitle")   to the [coarsened shared current time](h-entry "subtitle") given fetchParams’s   [cross-origin isolated capability](h-entry "subtitle").          
5.         Set response to the result of making an HTTP request over connection    using request with the following caveats:    
                 6.           Follow the relevant requirements from HTTP. [[HTTP]](h-entry "subtitle") [[HTTP-CACHING]](h-entry "subtitle")     
         
7.           If request’s [body](h-entry "subtitle") is non-null, and request’s      [body](h-entry "subtitle")’s [source](h-entry "subtitle") is null, then the user agent may have a      buffer of up to 64 kibibytes and store a part of request’s [body](h-entry "subtitle")      in that buffer. If the user agent reads from request’s [body](h-entry "subtitle")      beyond that buffer’s size and the user agent needs to resend request, then instead      return a [network error](h-entry "subtitle").      
          

           The resending is needed when the connection is timed out, for example.       
           
	The buffer is not needed when request’s [body](h-entry "subtitle")’s       [source](h-entry "subtitle") is non-null, because request’s [body](h-entry "subtitle") can       be recreated from it.       
           
	When request’s [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [source](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is null, it       means [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is created from a `[ReadableStream](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")` object, which means       [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") cannot be recreated and that is why the buffer is needed.      
                   
8.           While true:      
         

           1.             Set timingInfo’s       [final network-response start time](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to the       [coarsened shared current time](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") given fetchParams’s       [cross-origin isolated capability](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), immediately after the user       agent’s HTTP parser receives the first byte of the response (e.g., frame header bytes for       HTTP/2 or response status line for HTTP/1.x).       
           
2.             Wait until all the HTTP response headers are transmitted.       
           
3.             Run the [WebDriver BiDi response started](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") steps with       request and response.       
           
4.             Let status be the HTTP response’s status code.       
           
5.             If status is in the range 100 to 199, inclusive:        
           

             1.               If timingInfo’s         [first interim network-response start time](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is 0, then set         timingInfo’s         [first interim network-response start time](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to         timingInfo’s [final network-response start time](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").         
             
2.               If request’s [mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`websocket`" and         status is 101, then [break](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").         
             
3.               If status is 103 and fetchParams’s         [process early hints response](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is non-null, then         [queue a fetch task](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to run fetchParams’s         [process early hints response](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), with [response](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").         
             
4.               [Continue](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").        
            

            These kind of HTTP responses are eventually followed by a "final" HTTP        response.       
           
-             [Break](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").      
          

        
        The exact layering between Fetch and HTTP still needs to be sorted through and    therefore response represents both a [response](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") and    an HTTP response here.    
        
	If the HTTP request results in a TLS client certificate dialog, then:    
       

         1.           If request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is a     [traversable navigable](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then make the dialog available in request’s     [traversable for user prompts](# "subtitle").     
         
2.           Otherwise, return a [network error](# "subtitle").    
        

        To transmit request’s [body](# "subtitle") body, run these steps:    
       

         1.           If body is null and fetchParams’s     [process request end-of-body](# "subtitle") is non-null, then     [queue a fetch task](# "subtitle") given fetchParams’s     [process request end-of-body](# "subtitle") and fetchParams’s     [task destination](# "subtitle").     
         
2.           Otherwise, if body is non-null:      
         

           1.             Let processBodyChunk given bytes be these steps:        
           

             1.               If fetchParams is [canceled](# "subtitle"), then abort these         steps.         
             
2.               Run this step [in parallel](# "subtitle"): transmit bytes.         
             
3.               If fetchParams’s         [process request body chunk length](# "subtitle") is non-null, then run         fetchParams’s [process request body chunk length](# "subtitle") given         bytes’s [length](# "subtitle").        
            

           
-             Let processEndOfBody be these steps:        
           

             1.               If fetchParams is [canceled](# "subtitle"), then abort these         steps.         
             
2.               If fetchParams’s [process request end-of-body](# "subtitle") is         non-null, then run fetchParams’s         [process request end-of-body](# "subtitle").        
            

           
-             Let processBodyError given e be these steps:        
           

             1.               If fetchParams is [canceled](# "subtitle"), then abort these         steps.         
             
2.               If e is an "`[AbortError](# "subtitle")`" `[DOMException](# "subtitle")`,         then [abort](# "subtitle") fetchParams’s         [controller](# "subtitle").         
             
3.               Otherwise, [terminate](# "subtitle") fetchParams’s         [controller](# "subtitle").        
            

           
-             [Incrementally read](# "subtitle") request’s [body](# "subtitle") given       processBodyChunk, processEndOfBody, processBodyError, and       fetchParams’s [task destination](# "subtitle").      
          

        

      

     
-       
[If aborted](# "subtitle"), then:  
     

       1.         If connection uses HTTP/2, then transmit an `RST_STREAM` frame.   
       
2.         Return the [appropriate network error](# "subtitle") for fetchParams.  
      

     
-       
Let buffer be an empty [byte sequence](# "subtitle").  
      
This represents an internal buffer inside the network layer of the user agent. 
     
-       
Let stream be a [new](# "subtitle") `[ReadableStream](# "subtitle")`. 
     
-       
Let pullAlgorithm be the following steps:  
     

       1.         Let promise be [a new promise](# "subtitle").   
       
2.         Run the following steps [in parallel](# "subtitle"):    
       

         1.           If the size of buffer is smaller than a lower limit chosen by the user agent and the     ongoing fetch is [suspended](# "subtitle"), [resume](# "subtitle") the fetch.     
         
2.           Wait until buffer is not empty.     
         
3.           [Queue a fetch task](# "subtitle") to run the following steps, with fetchParams’s      [task destination](# "subtitle").      
         

           1.             [Pull from bytes](# "subtitle") buffer into stream.       
           
2.             If stream is [errored](# "subtitle"), then [terminate](# "subtitle")       fetchParams’s [controller](# "subtitle").       
           
3.             [Resolve](# "subtitle") promise with undefined.      
          

        

       
-         Return promise.  
      

     
-       
Let cancelAlgorithm be an algorithm that [aborts](# "subtitle") fetchParams’s [controller](# "subtitle") with
reason, given reason. 
     
-       
[Set up](# "subtitle") stream with byte reading support with [pullAlgorithm](# "subtitle") 
set to pullAlgorithm, [cancelAlgorithm](# "subtitle") set to cancelAlgorithm. 
     
-       
Set response’s [body](# "subtitle") to a new [body](# "subtitle") whose [stream](# "subtitle") is
stream. 
     
-       
Run the [WebDriver BiDi clone network response body](# "subtitle") steps with request 
and response. 
     
-       
[![WHATWG](https://resources.whatwg.org/commit-snapshot-shortcut-key.js "subtitle")](# "subtitle")
 If includeCredentials is true, then the user agent should [parse and store response \``Set-Cookie`\` 
headers](# "subtitle") given request and response. 
     
-       
Run these steps [in parallel](# "subtitle"):  
     

       1.         Run these steps, but [abort when](# "subtitle") fetchParams is    [canceled](# "subtitle"):    
       

         1.           While true:      
         

           1.             If one or more bytes have been transmitted from response’s message body, then:        
           

             1.               Let bytes be the transmitted bytes.         
             
2.               Let codings be the result of [extracting header list values](# "subtitle") given         \``Content-Encoding`\` and response’s [header list](# "subtitle").         
             
3.               Let filteredCoding be "`@unknown`".         
             
4.               If codings is null or failure, then set filteredCoding to         the empty string.         
             
5.               Otherwise, if codings’s [size](# "subtitle") is greater than 1, then set         filteredCoding to "`multiple`".         
             
6.               Otherwise, if codings[0] is the empty string, or it is supported by the         user agent, and is a [byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for an entry listed in the         *HTTP Content Coding Registry*, then set filteredCoding to the result         of [byte-lowercasing](# "subtitle") codings[0]. [[IANA-HTTP-PARAMS]](# "subtitle")         
             
7.               Set response’s [body info](# "subtitle")’s         [content encoding](# "subtitle") to filteredCoding.         
             
8.               Increase response’s [body info](# "subtitle")’s         [encoded size](# "subtitle") by bytes’s         [length](# "subtitle").         
             
9.               Set bytes to the result of [handling content          codings](# "subtitle") given codings and bytes.          
              
	This makes the \``Content-Length`\` [header](# "subtitle") unreliable          to the extent that it was reliable to begin with.         
             
10.               Increase response’s [body info](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s         [decoded size](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") by         bytes’s [length](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").         
             
11.               If bytes is failure, then [terminate](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")         fetchParams’s [controller](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").         
             
12.               Append bytes to buffer.         
             
13.               If the size of buffer is larger than an upper limit chosen by the user agent, ask         the user agent to [suspend](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") the ongoing fetch.        
            

           
-             Otherwise, if the bytes transmission for response’s message body is done       normally and stream is [readable](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"), then       [close](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") stream, and abort these in-parallel steps.      
          

        

       
-         [If aborted](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"), then:    
       

         1.           If fetchParams is [aborted](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"), then:      
         

           1.             Set response’s [aborted flag](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").       
           
2.             If stream is [readable](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"), then       [error](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") stream with the result of       [deserialize a serialized abort reason](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") given fetchParams’s       [controller](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s [serialized abort reason](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")       and an [implementation-defined](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") [realm](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").      
          

         
-           Otherwise, if stream is [readable](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"),     [error](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") stream with a `[TypeError](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")`.     
         
-           If connection uses HTTP/2, then transmit an `RST_STREAM` frame.     
         
-           Otherwise, the user agent should close connection unless it would be bad for      performance to do so.      
          
	For instance, the user agent could keep the connection open if it knows there’s      only a few bytes of transfer remaining on a reusable connection. In this case it could be      worse to close the connection and go through the handshake process again for the next fetch.    
        

      

      
These are run [in parallel](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") as at this point it is unclear whether 
response’s [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") is relevant (response 
might be a  redirect). 
     
-       
Return response. Typically response’s [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")’s [stream](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") is still being enqueued to after returning.
    

      
### 4.8. CORS-preflight fetch
   
This is effectively the user agent implementation of the check to see if the[CORS protocol](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") is understood. The so-called [CORS-preflight request](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle"). If successful itpopulates the [CORS-preflight cache](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") to minimize the number of these[fetches](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle").
   

    
To *CORS-preflight fetch*, given a [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")request, 
run these steps:
   

     1.       Let preflight be a new [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") whose  [method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is \``OPTIONS`\`,  [URL list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is a [clone](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") of request’s  [URL list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),  [initiator](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is request’s [initiator](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),  [destination](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is request’s [destination](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),  [origin](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is request’s [origin](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),  [referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is request’s [referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),  [referrer policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is request’s [referrer policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),  [mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is "`cors`",  [response tainting](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is "`cors`", and  [WebDriver id](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is request’s [WebDriver id](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").  
      
	The [service-workers mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") of preflight does not matter  as this algorithm uses [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") rather than [HTTP fetch](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). 
     
2.       [Append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") (\``Accept`\`, \``*/*`\`) to preflight’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). 
     
3.       [Append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") (\`[`Access-Control-Request-Method`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")\`, request’s [method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")) to preflight’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). 
     
4.       Let headers be the [CORS-unsafe request-header names](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") with request’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). 
     
5.       If headers [is not empty](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), then:  
     

       1.         Let value be the items in headers separated from each other by   \``,`\`.   
       
2.         [Append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")   (\`[`Access-Control-Request-Headers`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")\`, value) to   preflight’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").  
      

      This intentionally does not use [combine](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), as 0x20 following  0x2C is not the way this was implemented, for better or worse. 
     
-       Let response be the result of running [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") given a new [fetch params](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") whose [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is preflight. 
     
-       If a [CORS check](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for request and response returns success and  response’s [status](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is an [ok status](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), then:  
      
	The [CORS check](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is done on request rather than preflight  to ensure the correct [credentials mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is used.  
     

       1.         Let methods be the result of [extracting header list values](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") given   \`[`Access-Control-Allow-Methods`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")\` and response’s   [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   
       
2.         Let headerNames be the result of [extracting header list values](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") given   \`[`Access-Control-Allow-Headers`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")\` and response’s   [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   
       
3.         If either methods or headerNames is failure,   return a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   
       
4.         If methods is null and request’s [use-CORS-preflight flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")    is set, then set methods to a new list containing request’s    [method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    
        
	This ensures that a [CORS-preflight fetch](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") that happened due to    request’s [use-CORS-preflight flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") being set is    [cached](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   
       
5.         If request’s [method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is not in methods,   request’s [method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is not a [CORS-safelisted method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), and   request’s [credentials mode](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle") is "`include`" or   methods does not contain \``*`\`, then return a [network error](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key").   
       
6.         If one of request’s [header list](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues/new/choose "You can also press the 'y' key")’s   [names](https://whatwg.org/chat "You can also press the 'y' key") is a [CORS non-wildcard request-header name](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/commits "You can also press the 'y' key") and is not a   [byte-case-insensitive](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key") match for an [item](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese") in headerNames, then   return a [network error](https://triple-underscore.github.io/Fetch-ja.html "Japanese").   
       
7.         [For each](ja "Simplified Chinese") unsafeName of the   [CORS-unsafe request-header names](zh-hans "Korean") with request’s   [header list](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3"), if unsafeName is not a [byte-case-insensitive](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")   match for an [item](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") in headerNames and request’s   [credentials mode](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`include`" or headerNames does not   contain \``*`\`, return a [network error](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3").   
       
8.         Let max-age be the result of [extracting header list values](#http "Content Security Policy Level 3") given   \`[`Access-Control-Max-Age`](#methods "Content Security Policy Level 3")\` and response’s   [header list](#statuses "Content Security Policy Level 3").   
       
9.         If max-age is failure or null, then set max-age to 5.   
       
10.         If max-age is greater than an imposed limit on   [max-age](#responses "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then set max-age to the imposed limit.   
       
11.         If the user agent does not provide for a [cache](#authentication-entries "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then   return response.   
       
12.         For each method in methods for which there is a   [method cache entry match](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3") using request, set matching entry’s   [max-age](#connections "Content Security Policy Level 3") to max-age.   
       
13.         For each method in methods for which there is no   [method cache entry match](#http-cache-partitions "Content Security Policy Level 3") using request, [create a new cache entry](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3") with   request, max-age, method, and null.   
       
14.         For each headerName in headerNames for which there is a   [header-name cache entry match](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3") using request, set matching entry’s   [max-age](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3") to max-age.   
       
15.         For each headerName in headerNames for which there is no   [header-name cache entry match](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") using request, [create a new cache entry](#cookie-infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3")   with request, max-age, null, and headerName.   
       
16.         Return response.  
      

     
-       Otherwise, return a [network error](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3").
    

      
### 4.9. CORS-preflight cache
   
A user agent has an associated [CORS-preflight cache](#http-responses "Content Security Policy Level 3").
A*CORS-preflight cache* is a [list](#cors-protocol-examples "Content Security Policy Level 3") of [cache entries](#cors-protocol-examples "Content Security Policy Level 3").
   
A *cache entry* consists of:
   
    - *key* (a [network partition key](#x-content-type-options-header "Content Security Policy Level 3"))     
- *byte-serialized origin* (a [byte sequence](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3"))     
- *URL* (a [URL](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3"))     
- *max-age* (a number of seconds)     
- *credentials* (a boolean)     
- *method* (null, \``*`\`, or a [method](#http-network-or-cache-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3"))     
- *header name* (null, \``*`\`, or a [header name](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3"))   
   
[Cache entries](#cors-preflight-cache "Content Security Policy Level 3") must be removed after the seconds specified in their[max-age](#cors-check "Content Security Policy Level 3") field have passed since storing the entry. [Cache entries](#tao-check "Content Security Policy Level 3") maybe removed before that moment arrives.
   

    
To *create a new cache entry*, given request,max-age, method, and headerName, run 
these steps:
   

     1.       Let entry be a [cache entry](#headers-class "Content Security Policy Level 3"), initialized as follows:  
             [key](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3")                  The result of [determining the network partition key](#request-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") given   request   
       [byte-serialized origin](#fetch-method "Content Security Policy Level 3")                  The result of [byte-serializing a request origin](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3") with request   
       [URL](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3")                  request’s [current URL](#http-header-layer-division "Content Security Policy Level 3")   
       [max-age](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3")                  max-age   
       [credentials](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3")                  True if request’s [credentials mode](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level 3") is   "`include`", and false otherwise   
       [method](#fetch-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3")                  method   
       [header name](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3")                  headerName  
           
2.       [Append](#fetch-elsewhere-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") entry to the user agent’s [CORS-preflight cache](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3").
    

      
To *clear cache entries*, given a request,[remove](#index "Content Security Policy Level 3") any [cache entries](#index-defined-here "Content Security Policy Level 3") in the user agent’s [CORS-preflight cache](#index-defined-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3")whose [key](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") is the result of[determining the network partition key](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") given
request,[byte-serialized origin](#informative "Content Security Policy Level 3") is the result of[byte-serializing a request origin](#idl-index "Content Security Policy Level 3") with
request, and [URL](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3")is request’s 
[current URL](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3").
   
There is a *cache entry match* for a [cache entry](#biblio-fetch-metadata "Fetch Metadata Request
Headers")entry with request if entry’s [key](#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly") is theresult of [determining the network partition key](#biblio-mix "Mixed Content") given
request,entry’s [byte-serialized origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css) is the result of[byte-serializing a request origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css) with
request, entry’s[URL](h-entry "subtitle") is request’s [current URL](h-entry "subtitle"), and one of
   
    - entry’s [credentials](h-entry "subtitle") is true     
- entry’s [credentials](h-entry "subtitle") is false and request’s [credentials mode](h-entry "subtitle") is not "`include`".   
   
is true.
   
There is a *method cache entry match* formethod using request when there is a [cache entry](h-entry "subtitle") in the user agent’s[CORS-preflight cache](h-entry "subtitle") for which there is a [cache entry match](h-entry "subtitle") with
