requestand its [method](h-entry "subtitle") is method or \``*`\`.
   
There is a *header-name cache entry match* forheaderName using request when there 
is a [cache entry](h-entry "subtitle") in the useragent’s [CORS-preflight cache](h-entry "subtitle") for which there is a [cache entry match](h-entry "subtitle")
withrequest and one of
   
    - its [header name](h-entry "subtitle") is a [byte-case-insensitive](h-entry "subtitle") match for headerName     
- its [header name](h-entry "subtitle") is \``*`\` and headerName is not a [CORS non-wildcard request-header name](h-entry "subtitle")   
   
is true.
   
### 4.10. CORS check
   

    
To perform a *CORS check* for a request andresponse, run these steps:
   

     1.       Let origin be the result of [getting](h-entry "subtitle") \`[`Access-Control-Allow-Origin`](h-entry "subtitle")\` from response’s [header list](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
2.       If origin is null, then return failure.  
      
	Null is not \``null`\`. 
     
3.       If request’s [credentials mode](h-entry "subtitle") is not "`include`" and origin is \``*`\`, then return success. 
     
4.       If the result of [byte-serializing a request origin](h-entry "subtitle") with request is not origin, then return failure. 
     
5.       If request’s [credentials mode](h-entry "subtitle") is not "`include`", then return success. 
     
6.       Let credentials be the result of [getting](h-entry "subtitle") \`[`Access-Control-Allow-Credentials`](h-entry "subtitle")\` from response’s [header list](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
7.       If credentials is \``true`\`, then return success. 
     
8.       Return failure.
    

      
### 4.11. TAO check
   

    
To perform a *TAO check* for a request andresponse, run these steps:
   

     1.       [Assert](h-entry "subtitle"): request’s [origin](h-entry "subtitle") is not "`client`". 
     
2.       If request’s [timing allow failed flag](h-entry "subtitle") is set, then return failure. 
     
3.       Let values be the result of [getting, decoding, and splitting](h-entry "subtitle") \``Timing-Allow-Origin`\` from response’s [header list](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
4.       If values [contains](h-entry "subtitle") "`*`", then return success. 
     
5.       If values [contains](h-entry "subtitle") the result of [serializing a request origin](h-entry "subtitle") with request, then return success. 
     
6.       If request’s [mode](h-entry "subtitle") is "`navigate`" and  request’s [current URL](h-entry "subtitle")’s [origin](h-entry "subtitle") is not  [same origin](h-entry "subtitle") with request’s [origin](h-entry "subtitle"), then return failure.  
      
	This is necessary for navigations of a nested navigable. There,  request’s [origin](h-entry "subtitle") would be the container document’s  [origin](h-entry "subtitle") and the [TAO check](h-entry "subtitle") would return failure. Since navigation timing  never validates the results of the [TAO check](h-entry "subtitle"), the nested document would still have access  to the full timing information, but the container document would not. 
     
7.       If request’s [response tainting](h-entry "subtitle") is "`basic`", then return success. 
     
8.       Return failure.
    

      

    
To *append to a request’s navigation timing allow values list*, given a[request](h-entry "subtitle")
request and a [response](h-entry "subtitle") response:
   

     1.       [Assert](h-entry "subtitle"): request is a [navigation request](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
2.       Let taoValues be the result of [getting, decoding, and splitting](h-entry "subtitle") \``Timing-Allow-Origin`\` from response’s [header list](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
3.       If taoValues is null, then set taoValues to « ». 
     
4.       [Append](h-entry "subtitle") taoValues to request’s [navigation timing allow values list](h-entry "subtitle").
    

      

    
To perform a *navigation TAO check* for a[response](h-entry "subtitle") response 
and an [origin](h-entry "subtitle")destinationOrigin:
   

     1.       [For each](h-entry "subtitle") taoValues of response’s  [navigation timing allow values list](h-entry "subtitle"):  
     

       1.         If taoValues [contains](h-entry "subtitle") "`*`", then   [continue](h-entry "subtitle").   
       
2.         If taoValues [contains](h-entry "subtitle") destinationOrigin,   [serialized](h-entry "subtitle"), then [continue](h-entry "subtitle").   
       
3.         Return [failure](h-entry "subtitle").  
      

     
-       Return [success](h-entry "subtitle").
    

      
### 4.12. Deferred fetching
   
Deferred fetching allows callers to request that a fetch is invoked at the latest 
possiblemoment, i.e., when a [fetch group](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is [terminated](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), or after atimeout.
   
The *deferred fetch task source* is a [task source](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") used to update the result of adeferred fetch. User agents must prioritize tasks in this [task source](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") before other tasksources, specifically task sources that can result in running scripts such as the[DOM manipulation task source](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), to reflect the most recent state of a[`fetchLater()`](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") call before running any scripts that might depend on it.
   

    
To *queue a deferred fetch* given a [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")
request, a null or`[DOMHighResTimeStamp](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")` activateAfter, 
and onActivatedWithoutTermination,which is an algorithm that takes no arguments:
   

     1.       [Populate request from client](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") given request. 
     
2.       Set request’s [service-workers mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to "`none`". 
     
3.       Set request’s [keepalive](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to true. 
     
4.       Let deferredRecord be a new [deferred fetch record](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") whose [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is request, and whose [notify invoked](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is onActivatedWithoutTermination. 
     
5.       [Append](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") deferredRecord to request’s [client](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [fetch group](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [deferred fetch records](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"). 
     
6.       If activateAfter is non-null, then run the following steps [in parallel](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"):  
     

       1.         The user agent should wait until any of the following conditions is met:    
                 2.           At least activateAfter milliseconds have passed.     
         
3.           The user agent has a reason to believe that it is about to lose the opportunity to     execute scripts, e.g., when the browser is moved to the background, or when     request’s [client](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s     [global object](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is a `[Window](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")` object whose     [associated document](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") had a "`hidden`" [visibility state](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") for     a long period of time.    
        
       
4.         [Process](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") deferredRecord.  
      

     
-       
Return deferredRecord.
    

      

    
To compute the *total request length* of a [request](# "subtitle") request:
   

     1.       Let totalRequestLength be the [length](# "subtitle") of request’s [URL](# "subtitle"), [serialized](# "subtitle") with [*exclude fragment*](# "subtitle") set to true. 
     
2.       Increment totalRequestLength by the [length](# "subtitle") of request’s [referrer](# "subtitle"), [serialized](# "subtitle"). 
     
3.       [For each](# "subtitle") (name, value) of request’s [header list](# "subtitle"), increment totalRequestLength by name’s [length](# "subtitle") + value’s [length](# "subtitle"). 
     
4.       Increment totalRequestLength by request’s [body](# "subtitle")’s [length](# "subtitle"). 
     
5.       Return totalRequestLength.
    

      

    
To *process deferred fetches* given a [fetch group](# "subtitle") fetchGroup:
   

     1.       [For each](# "subtitle") [deferred fetch record](# "subtitle") deferredRecord of fetchGroup’s [deferred fetch records](# "subtitle"), [process a deferred fetch](# "subtitle") deferredRecord.
    

      

    
To *process a deferred fetch* deferredRecord:
   

     1.       If deferredRecord’s [invoke state](# "subtitle") is not "`pending`", then return. 
     
2.       Set deferredRecord’s [invoke state](# "subtitle") to "`sent`". 
     
3.       [Fetch](# "subtitle") deferredRecord’s [request](# "subtitle"). 
     
4.       [Queue a global task](# "subtitle") on the [deferred fetch task source](# "subtitle") with deferredRecord’s [request](# "subtitle")’s [client](# "subtitle")’s [global object](# "subtitle") to run deferredRecord’s [notify invoked](# "subtitle").
    

      
#### 4.12.1. Deferred fetching quota
   
*This section is non-normative.*
   
The deferred-fetch quota is allocated to a [top-level traversable](# "subtitle") (a "tab"),amounting to 640 kibibytes. The top-level document and its same-origin directly nested documents canuse this quota to queue deferred fetches, or delegate some of it to cross-origin nested documents,using permissions policy.
   
By default, 128 kibibytes out of these 640 kibibytes are allocated to delegating 
the quota tocross-origin nested documents, each reserving 8 kibibytes.
   
The top-level [document](# "subtitle"), and subsequently its nested documents, can control how muchof their quota is delegated to cross-origin child documents, using permissions policy. By default,the "`[deferred-fetch-minimal](#
"subtitle")`" policy is enabled for any origin, while"`[deferred-fetch](# "subtitle")`" is enabled 
for the top-level document’s origin only. Byrelaxing the "`[deferred-fetch](# "subtitle")`" policy 
for particular origins and nesteddocuments, the top-level document can allocate 64 
kibibytes to those nested documents. Similarly, byrestricting the "`[deferred-fetch-minimal](# "subtitle")`" 
policy for a particular origin ornested document, the document can prevent the document 
from reserving the 8 kibibytes it wouldreceive by default. By disabling the "`[deferred-fetch-minimal](# "subtitle")`" 
policy for thetop-level document itself, the entire 128 kibibytes delegated quota 
is collected back into the mainpool of 640 kibibytes.
   
Out of the allocated quota for a [document](# "subtitle"), only 64 kibibytes can be usedconcurrently for the same reporting origin (the [request](# "subtitle")’s [URL](# "subtitle")’s[origin](# "subtitle")). This prevents a situation where particular third-party libraries would reservequota opportunistically, before they have data to send.
   

         
Any of the following calls to [`fetchLater()`](# "subtitle") would throw due to the request itself exceeding the 64 kibibytes quota allocated to a reporting origin. Note that the size of the request includes the [URL](# "subtitle") itself, the [body](# "subtitle"), the [header list](# "subtitle"), and the [referrer](# "subtitle"). 

```h-entry status-LS
fetchLater(a_72_kb_url);
fetchLater("https://origin.example.com", {headers: headers_exceeding_64kb});
fetchLater(a_32_kb_url, {headers: headers_exceeding_32kb});
fetchLater("https://origin.example.com", {method: "POST", body: body_exceeding_64_kb});
fetchLater(a_62_kb_url /* with a 3kb referrer */);
```
    
In the following sequence, the first two requests would succeed, but the third one 
would throw. That’s because the overall 640 kibibytes quota was not exceeded in 
the first two calls, however the 3rd request exceeds the reporting-origin quota for 
`https://a.example.com`, and would throw. 

```h-entry status-LS
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {method: "POST", body: a_64kb_body});
fetchLater("https://b.example.com", {method: "POST", body: a_64kb_body});
fetchLater("https://a.example.com");
```
    
Same-origin nested documents share the quota of their parent. However, cross-origin 
or cross-agent iframes only receive 8kb of quota by default. So in the following 
example, the first three calls would succeed and the last one would throw. 

```h-entry status-LS
// In main page
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {method: "POST", body: a_64kb_body});

// In same-origin nested document
fetchLater("https://b.example.com", {method: "POST", body: a_64kb_body});

// In cross-origin nested document at https://fratop.example.com
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {body: a_5kb_body});
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {body: a_12kb_body});
```
    
To make the previous example not throw, the top-level document can delegate some 
of its quota to `https://fratop.example.com`, for example by serving the following header: 

```h-entry status-LS
Permissions-Policy: deferred-fetch=(self "https://fratop.example.com")```

Each nested document reserves its own quota. So the following would work, because
each frame reserves 8 kibibytes:

```h-entry status-LS
// In cross-origin nested document at https://fratop.example.com/frame-1
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {body: a_6kb_body});

// In cross-origin nested document at https://fratop.example.com/frame-2
fetchLater("https://a.example.com", {body: a_6kb_body});
```

The following tree illustrates how quota is distributed to different nested documents
in a tree:

-       `https://top.example.com`, with permissions policy set to   `Permissions-policy: deferred-fetch=(self "https://ok.example.com")`   
-         `https://top.example.com/frame`: shares quota with the top-level traversable, as     they are same origin.     
-           `https://x.example.com`: receives 8 kibibytes.


-         
`https://x.example.com`: receives 8 kibibytes.     

-           `https://top.example.com`: 0. Even though it’s same origin with the     top-level traversable, it does not automatically share its quota as they are separated by a     cross-origin intermediary.


-         
`https://ok.example.com/good`: receives 64 kibibytes, granted via the     "`[deferred-fetch](# "subtitle")`" policy.     

-           `https://x.example.com`: receives no quota. Only documents with the same     origin as the top-level traversable can grant the 8 kibibytes based on the     "`[deferred-fetch-minimal](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")`" policy.


-         
`https://ok.example.com/redirect`, navigated to    `https://x.example.com`: receives no quota. The reserved 64 kibibytes for    `https://ok.example.com` are
not available for    `https://x.example.com`.    

-         
`https://ok.example.com/back`, navigated to    `https://top.example.com`: shares quota with the top-level traversable, as they’re
same origin.   


In the above example, the [top-level traversable](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") and its [same origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") descendants share a quota of 384 kibibytes. That value is computed as such:

-       640 kibibytes are initially granted to the [top-level traversable](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").  

-       128 kibibytes are reserved for the "`[deferred-fetch-minimal](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")`" policy.  

-       64 kibibytes are reserved for the container navigating to  `https://ok.example/good`.  

-       64 kibibytes are reserved for the container navigating to  `https://ok.example/redirect`, and lost when it navigates away.  

- `https://ok.example.com/back` did not reserve 64 kibibytes, because it navigated  back to [top-level traversable](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s origin.       
-       640 − 128 − 64 − 64 = 384 kibibytes.


This specification defines a [policy-controlled feature](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") identified by the
string"*`deferred-fetch`*". Its[default allowlist](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") is
"`self`".

This specification defines a [policy-controlled feature](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") identified by the
string"*`deferred-fetch-minimal`*". Its[default allowlist](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") is
"`*`".

The *quota reserved for `deferred-fetch-minimal`* is 128 kibibytes.

Each [navigable container](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") has an associated
number*reserved deferred-fetch quota*. Its possible values are*minimal quota*, which
is 8 kibibytes, and*normal quota*, which is 0 or 64 kibibytes. Unlessstated otherwise,
it is 0.


To get the *available deferred-fetch quota* given a [document](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg
"subtitle")document and an [origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")-or-null
origin:


1.       Let controlDocument be document’s [deferred-fetch control document](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle").

2.       Let navigable be controlDocument’s [node navigable](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle").

3.       Let isTopLevel be true if controlDocument’s [node navigable](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is a [top-level traversable](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"); otherwise false.

4.       Let deferredFetchAllowed be true if controlDocument is [allowed to use](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") the [policy-controlled feature](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") "`[deferred-fetch](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`"; otherwise false.

5.       Let deferredFetchMinimalAllowed be true if controlDocument is [allowed to use](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") the [policy-controlled feature](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") "`[deferred-fetch-minimal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`"; otherwise false.

6.       Let quota be the result of the first matching statement:  
isTopLevel is true and deferredFetchAllowed is false          0          isTopLevel is true and deferredFetchMinimalAllowed is false                  640 kibibytes    

	640kb should be enough for everyone.   
isTopLevel is true                  512 kibibytes    

	The default of 640 kibibytes, decremented by    [quota reserved for `deferred-fetch-minimal`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   
deferredFetchAllowed is true, and navigable’s   [navigable container](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is   [normal quota](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")          [normal quota](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")          deferredFetchMinimalAllowed is true, and navigable’s   [navigable container](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is   [minimal quota](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")          [minimal quota](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")          Otherwise          0             
7.       Let quotaForRequestOrigin be 64 kibibytes.

8.       [For each](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") navigable in controlDocument’s  [node navigable](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [inclusive descendant navigables](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") whose  [active document](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [deferred-fetch control document](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is  controlDocument:  


1.         [For each](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") container in navigable’s   [active document](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [shadow-including inclusive descendants](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") which is a   [navigable container](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), decrement quota by container’s   [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   

2.         [For each](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [deferred fetch record](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") deferredRecord of    navigable’s [active document](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [relevant settings object](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s    [fetch group](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s    [deferred fetch records](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"):    


1.           If deferredRecord’s [invoke state](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")     is not "`pending`", then [continue](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     

2.           Let requestLength be the [total request length](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") of     deferredRecord’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     

3.           Decrement quota by requestLength.     

4.           If deferredRecord’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s     [URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [origin](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is [same origin](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") with origin,     then decrement quotaForRequestOrigin by requestLength.    


-       If quota is equal or less than 0, then return 0.

-       If quota is less than quotaForRequestOrigin, then return quota.

-       Return quotaForRequestOrigin.


To *reserve deferred-fetch quota* for a [navigable container](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")container given an [origin](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") originToNavigateTo:

This is called on navigation, when the source document of the navigation is the[navigable](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s parent document. It potentially reserves either 64kb or 8kb of quota forthe container and its navigable, if allowed by permissions policy. It is not observable to thecontainer document whether the reserved quota was used in practice. This algorithm assumes that thecontainer’s document might delegate quota to the navigated container, and the reserved quota wouldonly apply in that case, and would be ignored if it ends up being shared. If quota was reserved andthe document ends up being [same origin](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues/new/choose "You can also press the 'y' key") with its parent, the quota would be[freed](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/commits "You can also press the 'y' key").


1.       Set container’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key") to 0.

2.       Let controlDocument be container’s [node document](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese")’s [deferred-fetch control document](https://triple-underscore.github.io/Fetch-ja.html "Japanese").

3.       If the [inherited policy](https://htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Simplified Chinese") for "`[deferred-fetch](ko "Content Security Policy Level 3")`", container and originToNavigateTo is `"Enabled"`, and the [available deferred-fetch quota](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") for controlDocument is equal or greater than [normal quota](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then set container’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](#preface "Content Security Policy Level 3") to [normal quota](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3") and return.

4.       If all of the following conditions are true:  
5.         controlDocument’s [node navigable](#methods "Content Security Policy Level 3") is a [top-level traversable](#terminology-headers "Content Security Policy Level 3");   

6.         the [inherited policy](#bodies "Content Security Policy Level 3")   for "`[deferred-fetch-minimal](#responses "Content Security Policy Level 3")`", container and   originToNavigateTo is `"Enabled"`; and   

7.         the [size](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3") of controlDocument’s [node navigable](#resolving-domains "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s   [descendant navigables](#connections "Content Security Policy Level 3"), [removing](#http-cache-partitions "Content Security Policy Level 3") any [navigable](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3")   whose [navigable container](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-mime-type? "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not   [minimal quota](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3"), is less than   [quota reserved for `deferred-fetch-minimal`](#cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") /   [minimal quota](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3"),  


then set container’s [reserved deferred-fetch quota](#origin-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") to  [minimal quota](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3").


To *potentially free deferred-fetch quota* for a [document](#http-new-header-syntax "Content Security Policy Level
3")document, if document’s [node navigable](#cors-protocol-and-credentials "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [container document](#cors-protocol-examples "Content Security Policy Level 3") isnot null, and its [origin](#cors-protocol-exceptions "Content Security Policy Level 3") is [same origin](#content-length-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") with
document, thenset document’s [node navigable](#x-content-type-options-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [navigable container](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s[reserved deferred-fetch quota](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") to 0.

This is called when a [document](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") is created. It ensures that same-originnested documents don’t reserve quota, as they anyway share their parent quota. It can only be calledupon document creation, as the [origin](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3") of the [document](#main-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") is only knownafter redirects are handled.


To get the *deferred-fetch control document* of a [document](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level
3")document:


1.       If document’ [node navigable](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [container document](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") is null or a [document](#cors-check "Content Security Policy Level 3") whose [origin](#tao-check "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not [same origin](#deferred-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") with document, then return document; otherwise, return the [deferred-fetch control document](#fetch-api "Content Security Policy Level 3") given document’s [node navigable](#headers-class "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [container document](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3").


## 5. Fetch API

The [`fetch()`](#response-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") method is relatively low-level API for[fetching](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3") resources. It covers slightly more ground than `[XMLHttpRequest](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level
3")`,although it is currently lacking when it comes to request progression (not
response progression).


The [`fetch()`](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3") method makes it quite straightforward to [fetch](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3") a resource and extract its contents as a `[Blob](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level
3")`:

```secno
fetch("/music/pk/altes-kamuffel.flac")
  .then(res => res.blob()).then(playBlob)
```

If you just care to log a particular response header:

```toc
fetch("/", {method:"HEAD"})
  .then(res => log(res.headers.get("strict-transport-security")))
```

If you want to check a particular response header and then process the response of
a cross-origin resource:

```secno
fetch("https://pk.example/berlin-calling.json", {mode:"cors"})
  .then(res => {
    if(res.headers.get("content-type") &&
       res.headers.get("content-type").toLowerCase().indexOf("application/json") >= 0) {
      return res.json()
    } else {
      throw new TypeError()
    }
  }).then(processJSON)
```

If you want to work with URL query parameters:

```heading no-num settled short
var url = new URL("https://geo.example.org/api"),
    params = {lat:35.696233, long:139.570431}
Object.keys(params).forEach(key => url.searchParams.append(key, params[key]))
fetch(url).then(/* … */)
```

If you want to receive the body data progressively:

```heading settled short
function consume(reader) {
  var total = 0
  return pump()
  function pump() {
    return reader.read().then(({done, value}) => {
      if (done) {
        return
      }
      total += value.byteLength
      log(`received ${value.byteLength} bytes (${total} bytes in total)`)
      return pump()
    })
  }
}

fetch("/music/pk/altes-kamuffel.flac")
  .then(res => consume(res.body.getReader()))
  .then(() => log("consumed the entire body without keeping the whole thing in memory!"))
  .catch(e => log("something went wrong: " + e))
```

### 5.1. Headers class

```typedef ([sequence](h-entry "subtitle")<[sequence](h-entry "subtitle")<[ByteString](h-entry "subtitle")>>
or [record](h-entry "subtitle")<[ByteString](h-entry "subtitle"), [ByteString](h-entry "subtitle")>) *h-entry
HeadersInit*;[[Exposed](h-entry "subtitle")=(Window,Worker)]interface *h-entry
Headers* {  [constructor](h-entry "subtitle")(optional [HeadersInit](h-entry "subtitle") *h-entry
init*);  [undefined](h-entry "subtitle") [append](h-entry "subtitle")([ByteString](h-entry "subtitle") *h-entry
name*, [ByteString](h-entry "subtitle") *h-entry
value*);  [undefined](h-entry "subtitle") [delete](h-entry "subtitle")([ByteString](h-entry "subtitle") *h-entry
name*);  [ByteString](h-entry "subtitle")? [get](h-entry "subtitle")([ByteString](h-entry "subtitle") *h-entry
name*);  [sequence](h-entry "subtitle")<[ByteString](h-entry "subtitle")> [getSetCookie](h-entry "subtitle")(); 
[boolean](h-entry "subtitle") [has](h-entry "subtitle")([ByteString](h-entry "subtitle") *h-entry
name*);  [undefined](h-entry "subtitle") [set](h-entry "subtitle")([ByteString](h-entry "subtitle") *h-entry
name*, [ByteString](h-entry "subtitle") *h-entry
value*);  [iterable](h-entry "subtitle")<[ByteString](h-entry "subtitle"), [ByteString](h-entry "subtitle")>;};```
   
A `[Headers](h-entry "subtitle")` object has an associated*header list* (a[header list](h-entry "subtitle")), which is initially empty.
Thiscan be a pointer to the [header list](h-entry "subtitle") of something else, e.g.,of a [request](h-entry "subtitle") as demonstrated by `[Request](h-entry
"subtitle")`objects.
   
A `[Headers](h-entry "subtitle")` object also has an associated*guard*, which is a *headers 
guard*. A[headers guard](h-entry "subtitle") is "`immutable`", "`request`","`request-no-cors`", "`response`" or "`none`".
   
---
       `headers = new [Headers](h-entry "subtitle")([init])`          
Creates a new `[Headers](h-entry "subtitle")` object. init can be used to fill its internal 
header list,  as per the example below.  
     

         

```h-entry
const meta = { "Content-Type": "text/xml", "Breaking-Bad": "<3" };
new Headers(meta);

// The above is equivalent to
const meta2 = [
[ "Content-Type", "text/xml" ],
[ "Breaking-Bad", "<3" ]
];
new Headers(meta2);
```
         `headers . [append](h-entry "subtitle")(name, value)`          
Appends a header to headers. 
    `headers . [delete](h-entry "subtitle")(name)`          
Removes a header from headers. 
    `headers . [get](h-entry "subtitle")(name)`          
Returns as a string the values of all headers whose name is name, separated by a 
comma and a space. 
    `headers . [getSetCookie](h-entry "subtitle")()`          
Returns a list of the values for all headers whose name is \``Set-Cookie`\`. 
    `headers . [has](h-entry "subtitle")(name)`          
Returns whether there is a header whose name is name. 
    `headers . [set](h-entry "subtitle")(name, value)`          
Replaces the value of the first header whose name is name with value and removes 
any remaining headers whose name is name. 
    `for(const [name, value] of headers)`          
headers can be iterated over.
      
---
   

    
To *validate* a  [header](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") (name, 
value) fora `[Headers](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")` object headers:
   

     1.       If name is not a [header name](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") or value is not a [header value](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then [throw](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`. 
     
2.       If headers’s [guard](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`immutable`", then [throw](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`. 
     
3.       If headers’s [guard](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`request`" and (name, value) is a [forbidden request-header](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then return false. 
     
4.       If headers’s [guard](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`response`" and name is a [forbidden response-header name](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then return false. 
     
5.       Return true.
    

      
Steps for "`request-no-cors`" are not shared as you cannot have a fakevalue (for `[delete()](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css
"subtitle")`) that always succeeds in [CORS-safelisted request-header](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").
   

    
To *append* a [header](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")(name, 
value) to a `[Headers](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")` object headers, 
run these steps:
   

     1.       [Normalize](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") value. 
     
2.       If [validating](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") (name, value) for headers returns false, then return. 
     
3.       If headers’s [guard](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`request-no-cors`":  
     

       1.         Let temporaryValue be the result of [getting](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")   name from headers’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").   
       
2.         If temporaryValue is null, then set temporaryValue to   value.   
       
3.         Otherwise, set temporaryValue to temporaryValue, followed by   0x2C 0x20, followed by value.   
       
4.         If (name, temporaryValue) is not a   [no-CORS-safelisted request-header](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then return.  
      

     
-       [Append](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") (name, value) to headers’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"). 
     
-       If headers’s [guard](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`request-no-cors`", then [remove privileged no-CORS request-headers](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") from headers.
    

      

    
To *fill* a `[Headers](# "subtitle")` objectheaders with a given object object, run these 
steps:
   

     1.       If object is a [sequence](# "subtitle"), then [for each](# "subtitle") header of  object:  
     

       1.         If header’s [size](# "subtitle") is not 2, then [throw](# "subtitle") a `[TypeError](# "subtitle")`.   
       
2.         [Append](# "subtitle") (header[0], header[1]) to   headers.  
      

     
-       Otherwise, object is a [record](# "subtitle"), then [for each](# "subtitle") key → value of object, [append](# "subtitle") (key, value) to headers.
    

      

    
To*remove privileged no-CORS request-headers*from a `[Headers](# "subtitle")` object (headers), 
run these steps:
   

     1.       [For each](# "subtitle") headerName of [privileged no-CORS request-header names](# "subtitle"): 
     

       1.         [Delete](# "subtitle") headerName from headers’s  [header list](# "subtitle"). 
      

    

    
This is called when headers are modified by unprivileged code.
      

    
The*`new Headers(init)`*constructor steps are:
   

     1.       Set [this](# "subtitle")’s [guard](# "subtitle") to "`none`". 
     
2.       If init is given, then [fill](# "subtitle") [this](# "subtitle") with init.
    

      

    
The *`append(name, value)`*method steps are to [append](# "subtitle") (name, value) to [this](# "subtitle").
      

    
The *`delete(name)`* method steps are:
   

     1.       If [validating](# "subtitle") (name, \`\`) for [this](# "subtitle") returns false, then  return.  
      
	Passing a dummy [header value](# "subtitle") ought not to have any negative repercussions. 
     
2.       If [this](# "subtitle")’s [guard](# "subtitle") is "`request-no-cors`", name is not a [no-CORS-safelisted request-header name](# "subtitle"), and name is not a [privileged no-CORS request-header name](# "subtitle"), then return. 
     
3.       If [this](# "subtitle")’s [header list](# "subtitle") [does not contain](# "subtitle") name, then return. 
     
4.       [Delete](# "subtitle") name from [this](# "subtitle")’s [header list](# "subtitle"). 
     
5.       If [this](# "subtitle")’s [guard](# "subtitle") is "`request-no-cors`", then [remove privileged no-CORS request-headers](# "subtitle") from [this](# "subtitle").
    

      

    
The *`get(name)`* method steps are:
   

     1.       If name is not a [header name](# "subtitle"), then [throw](# "subtitle") a `[TypeError](# "subtitle")`. 
     
2.       Return the result of [getting](# "subtitle") name from [this](# "subtitle")’s [header list](# "subtitle").
    

      

    
The *`getSetCookie()`* method steps are:
   

     1.       If [this](# "subtitle")’s [header list](# "subtitle") [does not contain](# "subtitle") \``Set-Cookie`\`, then return « ». 
     
2.       Return the [values](# "subtitle") of all [headers](# "subtitle") in [this](# "subtitle")’s [header list](# "subtitle") whose [name](# "subtitle") is a [byte-case-insensitive](# "subtitle") match for \``Set-Cookie`\`, in order.
    

      

    
The *`has(name)`* method steps are:
   

     1.       If name is not a [header name](# "subtitle"), then [throw](# "subtitle") a `[TypeError](# "subtitle")`. 
     
2.       Return true if [this](# "subtitle")’s [header list](# "subtitle") [contains](# "subtitle") name; otherwise false.
    

      

    
The *`set(name, value)`*method steps are:
   

     1.       [Normalize](# "subtitle") value. 
     
2.       If [validating](# "subtitle") (name, value) for [this](# "subtitle") returns false, then return. 
     
3.       If [this](# "subtitle")’s [guard](# "subtitle") is "`request-no-cors`" and (name, value) is not a [no-CORS-safelisted request-header](# "subtitle"), then return. 
     
4.       [Set](# "subtitle") (name, value) in [this](# "subtitle")’s [header list](# "subtitle"). 
     
5.       If [this](# "subtitle")’s [guard](# "subtitle") is "`request-no-cors`", then [remove privileged no-CORS request-headers](# "subtitle") from [this](# "subtitle").
    

      
The [value pairs to iterate over](# "subtitle") are the return value of running[sort and combine](# "subtitle") with [this](# "subtitle")’s [header list](# "subtitle").
   
### 5.2. BodyInit unions

```typedef ([Blob](# "subtitle") or [BufferSource](# "subtitle") or [FormData](# "subtitle") or
[URLSearchParams](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") or [USVString](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")) *h-entry status-LS
XMLHttpRequestBodyInit*;typedef ([ReadableStream](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") or
[XMLHttpRequestBodyInit](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")) *h-entry status-LS
BodyInit*;```

To *safely extract* a [body with type](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") from a[byte sequence](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") or `[BodyInit](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")`
object object, run these steps:


1.      If object is a `[ReadableStream](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")` object, then:  


1.        [Assert](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"): object is neither [disturbed](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") nor   [locked](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").  


-      Return the result of [extracting](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") object.


The [safely extract](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") operation is a subset of the[extract](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") operation that is guaranteed to not throw an exception.

To *extract* a[body with type](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") from a [byte sequence](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") or `[BodyInit](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")`
objectobject, with an optional boolean*keepalive* (default false), run thesesteps:


1.      Let stream be null.

2.      If object is a `[ReadableStream](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")` object, then set stream to object.

3.      Otherwise, if object is a `[Blob](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")` object, set stream to the result of running object’s [get stream](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle").

4.      Otherwise, set stream to a [new](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") `[ReadableStream](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")` object, and [set up](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") stream with byte reading support.

5.      [Assert](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle"): stream is a `[ReadableStream](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")` object.

6.      Let action be null.

7.      Let source be null.

8.      Let length be null.

9.      Let type be null.

10.      Switch on object:  
`[Blob](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")`                Set source to object.    

	Set length to object’s `[size](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")`.    

	If object’s `[type](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")`    attribute is not the empty byte sequence, set type to its value.   
[byte sequence](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")                Set source to object.   
`[BufferSource](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`                Set source to a [copy of the bytes](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")   held by object.   
`[FormData](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`                Set action to this step: run the    [`multipart/form-data` encoding algorithm](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), with object’s    [entry list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") and [UTF-8](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    

	Set source to object.    

	Set length to unclear, see    [html/6424](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for improving this.    

	Set type to \``multipart/form-data; boundary=`\`, followed by the    [`multipart/form-data` boundary string](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") generated by the    [`multipart/form-data` encoding algorithm](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   
`[URLSearchParams](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`                Set source to the result of running the    [`application/x-www-form-urlencoded` serializer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") with    object’s [list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    

	Set type to    \``application/x-www-form-urlencoded;charset=UTF-8`\`.   
[scalar value string](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")                Set source to the [UTF-8 encoding](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") of object.    

	Set type to \``text/plain;charset=UTF-8`\`.   
`[ReadableStream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`                If keepalive is true, then [throw](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.    

	If object is [disturbed](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") or    [locked](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), then [throw](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.  

11.      If source is a [byte sequence](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), then set action to a step that returns source and length to source’s [length](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

12.      If action is non-null, then run these steps [in parallel](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"):  


1.        Run action.    

	Whenever one or more bytes are available and stream is not    [errored](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), [enqueue](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") the result of    [creating](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[Uint8Array](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` from the available bytes into    stream.    

	When running action is done, [close](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") stream.  


-      Let body be a [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") whose [stream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is stream, [source](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is source, and [length](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is length.

-      Return (body, type).


### 5.3. Body mixin

```interface mixin *h-entry status-LS
Body* {  readonly attribute [ReadableStream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")? [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"); 
readonly attribute [boolean](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [bodyUsed](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"); 
[[NewObject](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")] [Promise](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")<[ArrayBuffer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")> [arrayBuffer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")(); 
[[NewObject](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")] [Promise](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")<[Blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")>
[blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")();  [[NewObject](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")] [Promise](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")<[Uint8Array](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")> [bytes](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")(); 
[[NewObject](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle")] [Promise](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y'
key")<[FormData](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key")> [formData](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues "You can also press the 'y' key")(); 
[[NewObject](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/commits "You can also press the 'y' key")] [Promise](/commit-snapshots/394d20d144ed1401c2c0e02c35bc3608cb2a2269/ "You can also press the 'y'
key")<[any](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "Japanese")> [json](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese")(); 
[[NewObject](https://triple-underscore.github.io/Fetch-ja.html "Japanese")] [Promise](https://htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Simplified
Chinese")<[USVString](https://ko.htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Korean")> [text](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")(); 
[[NewObject](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")] [ReadableStream](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")
[textStream](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")();};```
   
Formats you would not want a network layer to be dependent upon, such asHTML, will 
likely not be exposed here. Rather, an HTML parser API might accept a stream indue 
course.
   
Objects including the `[Body](#url "Content Security Policy Level 3")` interface mixin 
have an associated*body* (null or a [body](#terminology-headers "Content Security Policy Level 3")).
   
An object including the `[Body](#statuses "Content Security Policy Level 3")` interface 
mixin is said to be*unusable* if its [body](#responses "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null and its[body](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [stream](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3") is [disturbed](#authentication-entries "Content Security Policy Level 3") or[locked](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3").
   
---
       `requestOrResponse . [body](#connections "Content Security Policy Level 3")`          
Returns requestOrResponse’s body as `[ReadableStream](#http-cache-partitions "Content Security Policy Level
3")`. 
    `requestOrResponse . [bodyUsed](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3")`          
Returns whether requestOrResponse’s body has been read from. 
    `requestOrResponse . [arrayBuffer](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `[ArrayBuffer](#cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level
3")`. 
    `requestOrResponse . [blob](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `[Blob](#origin-header "Content Security Policy Level
3")`. 
    `requestOrResponse . [bytes](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `[Uint8Array](#general "Content Security Policy Level
3")`. 
    `requestOrResponse . [formData](#http-responses "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `[FormData](#cors-protocol-and-credentials "Content Security Policy Level
3")`. 
    `requestOrResponse . [json](#cors-protocol-examples "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body parsed as JSON. 
    `requestOrResponse . [text](#content-length-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as string. 
    `requestOrResponse . [textStream](#x-content-type-options-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`       
   
Returns a `[ReadableStream](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
object with requestOrResponse’s body as string chunks.
      
---
   

    
To *get the MIME type*, given a `[Request](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
or`[Response](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object requestOrResponse:
   

     1.       Let headers be null. 
     
2.       If requestOrResponse is a `[Request](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object, then set headers to requestOrResponse’s [request](#http-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [header list](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
3.       Otherwise, set headers to requestOrResponse’s [response](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [header list](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
4.       Let mimeType be the result of [extracting a MIME type](#cors-preflight-cache "Content Security Policy Level 3") from headers. 
     
5.       If mimeType is failure, then return null. 
     
6.       Return mimeType.
    

      

    
The *`body`* getter steps are to return null if[this](#fetch-api "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [body](#headers-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") is null; otherwise [this](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [body](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s[stream](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3").
      

    
The *`bodyUsed`* getter steps are to return true if[this](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [body](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null and [this](#background-reading "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [body](#http-header-layer-division "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s[stream](#http-header-layer-division "Content Security Policy Level 3") is [disturbed](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3"); otherwise false.
      

    
The *consume body*algorithm, given an object that includes `[Body](#fetch-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
object and an algorithm that takes a[byte sequence](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3") and returns a JavaScript value or throws an
exceptionconvertBytesToJSValue, runs these steps:
   

     1.       If object is [unusable](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then return [a promise rejected with](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3") a `[TypeError](#ipr "Content Security Policy Level 3")`. 
     
2.       Let promise be [a new promise](#index "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
3. Let errorSteps given error be to [reject](#index-defined-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3") promise with error.      
4. Let successSteps given a [byte sequence](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") data be to [resolve](#normative "Content Security Policy Level 3") promise with the result of running convertBytesToJSValue with data. If that threw an exception, then run errorSteps with that exception.      
5.       If object’s [body](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") is null, then run successSteps with an empty [byte sequence](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
6.       Otherwise, [fully read](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") object’s [body](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") given successSteps, errorSteps, and object’s [relevant global object](#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly"). 
     
7.       Return promise.
    

      

    
The *`arrayBuffer()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css) with [this](h-entry "subtitle") and the following step given a[byte sequence](h-entry "subtitle")
bytes: return the result of [creating](h-entry "subtitle") an`[ArrayBuffer](h-entry "subtitle")` 
from bytes in [this](h-entry "subtitle")’s [relevant realm](h-entry "subtitle").
    
The above method can reject with a `[RangeError](h-entry "subtitle")`.
      

    
The *`blob()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](h-entry "subtitle") with [this](h-entry "subtitle") and the following step given a[byte sequence](h-entry "subtitle")
bytes: return a `[Blob](h-entry "subtitle")` whose contents are bytesand whose `[type](h-entry "subtitle")` 
attribute is the result of [get the MIME type](h-entry "subtitle") with[this](h-entry "subtitle").
      

    
The *`bytes()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](h-entry "subtitle") with [this](h-entry "subtitle") and the following step given a[byte sequence](h-entry "subtitle")
bytes: return the result of [creating](h-entry "subtitle") a`[Uint8Array](h-entry "subtitle")` 
from bytes in [this](h-entry "subtitle")’s [relevant realm](h-entry "subtitle").
    
The above method can reject with a `[RangeError](h-entry "subtitle")`.
      

    
The *`formData()`* method steps are to return the result ofrunning [consume body](h-entry "subtitle") with [this](h-entry "subtitle") and the following steps given a[byte sequence](h-entry "subtitle")
bytes:
   

     1.       Let mimeType be the result of [get the MIME type](h-entry "subtitle") with [this](h-entry "subtitle"). 
     
2.       If mimeType is non-null, then switch on mimeType’s  [essence](h-entry "subtitle") and run the corresponding steps:  
             "`multipart/form-data`"                 

         1.           Parse bytes, using the value of the \``boundary`\` parameter from      mimeType, per the rules set forth in      *Returning Values from Forms: multipart/form-data*. [[RFC7578]](h-entry "subtitle")      
          
	Each part whose \``Content-Disposition`\` header contains a      \``filename`\` parameter must be parsed into an [entry](h-entry "subtitle") whose      value is a `[File](h-entry "subtitle")` object whose contents are the contents of the part. The `[name](h-entry "subtitle")`      attribute of the `[File](h-entry "subtitle")` object must have the value of the \``filename`\` parameter      of the part. The `[type](h-entry "subtitle")` attribute of the `[File](h-entry "subtitle")` object must have the value of the      \``Content-Type`\` header of the part if the part has such header, and      \``text/plain`\` (the default defined by [[RFC7578]](h-entry "subtitle") section 4.4) otherwise.      
          
	Each part whose \``Content-Disposition`\` header does not contain a      \``filename`\` parameter must be parsed into an [entry](h-entry "subtitle") whose      value is the [UTF-8 decoded without BOM](h-entry "subtitle") content of the      part. This is done regardless of the presence or the value of a      \``Content-Type`\` header and regardless of the presence or the value of a      \``charset`\` parameter.      
          
	A part whose \``Content-Disposition`\` header contains a      \``name`\` parameter whose value is \``_charset_`\` is parsed like any other      part. It does not change the encoding.     
         
2.           If that fails for some reason, then [throw](h-entry "subtitle") a `[TypeError](h-entry "subtitle")`.     
         
3.           Return a new `[FormData](h-entry "subtitle")` object, appending each [entry](h-entry "subtitle"),     resulting from the parsing operation, to its [entry list](h-entry "subtitle").    
        

        The above is a rough approximation of what is needed for    \``multipart/form-data`\`, a more detailed parsing specification is to be written.    Volunteers welcome.   
       "`application/x-www-form-urlencoded`"                 

         1.           Let entries be the result of  [parsing](h-entry "subtitle")     bytes.     
         
2.           Return a new `[FormData](h-entry "subtitle")` object whose [entry list](h-entry "subtitle") is     entries.    
        

           
-       [Throw](h-entry "subtitle") a `[TypeError](h-entry "subtitle")`.
    

      

    
The *`json()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](h-entry "subtitle") with [this](h-entry "subtitle") and [parse JSON from bytes](h-entry "subtitle").
    
The above method can reject with a `[SyntaxError](h-entry "subtitle")`.
      

    
The *`text()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](h-entry "subtitle") with [this](h-entry "subtitle") and [UTF-8 decode](h-entry "subtitle").
      

    
The *`textStream()`* method steps are:
   

     1.       If this is [unusable](h-entry "subtitle"), then throw a `[TypeError](h-entry "subtitle")`. 
     
2.       If this’s [body](h-entry "subtitle") is null:  
     

       1.         Let emptyStream be a new `[ReadableStream](h-entry "subtitle")` in [this](h-entry "subtitle")’s   [relevant realm](h-entry "subtitle").   
       
2.         [Set up](h-entry "subtitle") emptyStream.   
       
3.         [Close](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") emptyStream.   
       
4.         Return emptyStream.  
      

     
-       Let stream be [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [stream](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"). 
     
-       Let decoder be a new `[TextDecoderStream](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")` object in [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")’s [relevant realm](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"). 
     
-       [Set up](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") decoder with  [UTF-8](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").  
      
	This is done regardless of the presence or the value of a  \``Content-Type`\` header and regardless of the presence or the value of a  \``charset`\` parameter.
     
-       Return the result of stream, [piped through](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") decoder.
    

      
### 5.4. Request class
