"`serviceworker`" is omitted from[`RequestDestination`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") as it cannot be observed from JavaScript. Implementationswill still need to support it as a [destination](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
"`websocket`" and"`webtransport`" are omitted from [`RequestMode`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") as they cannot beused or observed from JavaScript.

A `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object has an associated*request* (a [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")).

A `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object also has an associated *headers* (null
or a`[Headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object), initially null.

A `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object has an associated *signal* (null or
an `[AbortSignal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`object), initially null.

A `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object’s [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is its[request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s[body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

---
`request = new [Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")(input [,
init])`          
Returns a new request whose `[url](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` property is input
if  input is a string, and input’s `[url](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` if input
is a  `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object.  

The init argument is an object whose properties can be set as follows:
`[method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string to set request’s
`[method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.         `[headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`
A `[Headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object, an object literal, or an array
of two-item arrays to set   request’s `[headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.    
`[body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A `[BodyInit](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`
object or null to set request’s [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").         `[referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`
A string whose value is a same-origin URL, "`about:client`", or the empty string,   to
set request’s [referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").         `[referrerPolicy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`
A [referrer policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to set request’s `[referrerPolicy](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")`.         `[mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string to indicate
whether the request will use CORS, or will be restricted to same-origin   URLs. Sets
request’s `[mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`. If input is a string, it defaults
to   "`cors`".         `[credentials](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string indicating
whether credentials will be sent with the request always, never, or only   when sent
to a same-origin URL — as well as whether any credentials sent back in the response
will be used always, never, or only when received from a same-origin URL. Sets
request’s `[credentials](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`. If input is a string, it defaults
to   "`same-origin`".         `[cache](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string indicating
how the request will interact with the browser’s cache to set   request’s `[cache](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")`.         `[redirect](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string indicating
whether request follows redirects, results in an error upon   encountering a redirect,
or returns the redirect (in an opaque fashion). Sets   request’s `[redirect](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")`.         `[integrity](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A cryptographic
hash of the resource to be fetched by request. Sets   request’s `[integrity](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")`.         `[keepalive](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A boolean to
set request’s `[keepalive](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle")`.         `[signal](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key")`
An `[AbortSignal](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key")`
to set request’s `[signal](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues "You can also press the 'y' key")`.
`[window](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/commits "You can also press the 'y' key")`
Can only be null. Used to disassociate request from any `[Window](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "You can also press the 'y'
key")`.         `[duplex](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese")`
"`half`" is the only valid value and it is for initiating a half-duplex fetch
(i.e., the user agent sends the entire request before processing the response).
"`full`" is reserved for future use, for initiating a full-duplex fetch (i.e., the
user agent can process the response before sending the entire request). This member
needs to be   set when `[body](https://ko.htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
is a `[ReadableStream](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object. See   [issue #1254](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") for defining  
"`full`".         `[priority](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")`         A string
to set request’s [priority](#preface "Content Security Policy Level 3").        
`request . [method](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns request’s
HTTP method, which is "`GET`" by default.     `request . [url](#http "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns the URL of request as a string.     `request . [headers](#terminology-headers "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns a `[Headers](#bodies "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object consisting of
the headers associated with request. Note that headers added in the network layer
by the user agent will not be accounted for in this object, e.g., the "`Host`" header.
`request . [destination](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns the kind of
resource requested by request, e.g., "`document`" or "`script`".     `request . [referrer](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns the referrer of request. Its value can be a same-origin URL if explicitly
set in init, the empty string to indicate no referrer, and "`about:client`" when defaulting to
the global’s default. This is used during fetching to determine the value of the
\``Referer`\` header of the request being made.     `request . [referrerPolicy](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns the referrer policy associated with request. This is used during fetching
to compute the value of the request’s referrer.     `request . [mode](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns the [mode](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3") associated with request,
which is a string indicating whether the request will use CORS, or will be restricted
to same-origin URLs.     `request . [credentials](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns the [credentials mode](#cookie-infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3") associated with
request, which is a string indicating whether credentials will be sent with the
request always, never, or only when sent to a same-origin URL.     `request . [cache](#general "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns the [cache mode](#http-requests "Content Security Policy Level 3") associated with
request, which is a string indicating how the request will interact with the browser’s
cache when fetching.     `request . [redirect](#http-new-header-syntax "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns the [redirect mode](#cors-protocol-and-credentials "Content Security Policy Level 3") associated with
request, which is a string indicating how redirects for the request will be handled
during fetching. A [request](#cors-protocol-exceptions "Content Security Policy Level 3") will follow redirects by default.     `request . [integrity](#content-length-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns request’s subresource integrity metadata, which is a cryptographic
hash of the resource being fetched. Its value consists of multiple hashes separated
by whitespace. [[SRI]](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")     `request . [keepalive](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns a boolean indicating whether or not request can outlive the global in
which it was created.     `request . [isReloadNavigation](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
Returns a boolean indicating whether or not request is for a reload navigation.
`request . [isHistoryNavigation](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns a boolean indicating
whether or not request is for a history navigation (a.k.a. back-foward navigation).
`request . [signal](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns the signal
associated with request, which is an `[AbortSignal](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
object indicating whether or not request has been aborted, and its abort event handler.
`request . [duplex](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns "`half`",
meaning the fetch will be half-duplex (i.e., the user agent sends the entire request
before processing the response). In future, it could also return "`full`", meaning the
fetch will be full-duplex (i.e., the user agent can process the response before sending
the entire request) to indicate that the fetch will be full-duplex. See [issue #1254](#deferred-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") for defining
"`full`".     `request . [clone](#deferred-fetch-quota "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`     

Returns a clone of request.

---


To *create* a `[Request](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object, given a[request](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3")
request, [headers guard](#request-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") guard,`[AbortSignal](#fetch-method "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
object signal, and [realm](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3") realm:


1.       Let requestObject be a [new](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3") `[Request](#background-reading "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object with realm.

2.       Set requestObject’s [request](#http-header-layer-division "Content Security Policy Level 3") to request.

3.       Set requestObject’s [headers](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3") to a [new](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3") `[Headers](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object with realm, whose [headers list](#websocket-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") is request’s [headers list](#fetch-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3") and [guard](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3") is guard.

4.       Set requestObject’s [signal](#fetch-elsewhere-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") to signal.

5.       Return requestObject.


---


The*`new Request(input, init)`*constructor steps are:


1.       Let request be null.

2.       Let fallbackMode be null.

3.       Let baseURL be [this](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [relevant settings object](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [API base URL](#informative "Content Security Policy Level 3").

4.       Let signal be null.

5.       If input is a string, then:  


1.         Let parsedURL be the result of   [parsing](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") input with   baseURL.   

2.         If parsedURL is failure, then [throw](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") a `[TypeError](#biblio-fetch-metadata "Content Security Policy Level 3")`.   

3.         If parsedURL [includes credentials](#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly"), then   [throw](#biblio-mix "Mixed Content") a `[TypeError](#biblio-referrer "Upgrade Insecure Requests")`.   

4.         Set request to a new [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css) whose [URL](h-entry "subtitle") is   parsedURL.   

5.         Set fallbackMode to "`cors`".  


-       Otherwise:  


1.         [Assert](h-entry "subtitle"): input is a `[Request](h-entry "subtitle")` object.   

2.         Set request to input’s   [request](h-entry "subtitle").   

3.         Set signal to input’s [signal](h-entry "subtitle").  


-       Let origin be [this](h-entry "subtitle")’s [relevant settings object](h-entry "subtitle")’s [origin](h-entry "subtitle").

-       Let traversableForUserPrompts be "`client`".

-       If request’s [traversable for user prompts](h-entry "subtitle") is an [environment settings object](h-entry "subtitle") and its [origin](h-entry "subtitle") is [same origin](h-entry "subtitle") with origin, then set traversableForUserPrompts to request’s [traversable for user prompts](h-entry "subtitle").

-       If init["`[window](h-entry "subtitle")`"] [exists](h-entry "subtitle") and is non-null, then [throw](h-entry "subtitle") a `[TypeError](h-entry "subtitle")`.

-       If init["`[window](h-entry "subtitle")`"] [exists](h-entry "subtitle"), then set traversableForUserPrompts to "`no-traversable`".

-       Set request to a new [request](h-entry "subtitle") with the following properties:  
[URL](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [URL](h-entry "subtitle").          [method](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [method](h-entry "subtitle").          [header list](h-entry "subtitle")          A copy of request’s [header list](h-entry "subtitle").          [unsafe-request flag](h-entry "subtitle")          Set.          [client](h-entry "subtitle")          [This](h-entry "subtitle")’s [relevant settings object](h-entry "subtitle").          [traversable for user prompts](h-entry "subtitle")          traversableForUserPrompts.          [internal priority](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [internal priority](h-entry "subtitle").          [origin](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [origin](h-entry "subtitle"). The propagation of the   [origin](h-entry "subtitle") is only significant for navigation requests being handled by a service   worker. In this scenario a request can have an origin that is different from the current   client.          [referrer](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [referrer](h-entry "subtitle").          [referrer policy](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [referrer policy](h-entry "subtitle").          [mode](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [mode](h-entry "subtitle").          [credentials mode](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [credentials mode](h-entry "subtitle").          [cache mode](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [cache mode](h-entry "subtitle").          [redirect mode](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [redirect mode](h-entry "subtitle").          [integrity metadata](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [integrity metadata](h-entry "subtitle").          [keepalive](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [keepalive](h-entry "subtitle").          [reload-navigation flag](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [reload-navigation flag](h-entry "subtitle").          [history-navigation flag](h-entry "subtitle")          request’s [history-navigation flag](h-entry "subtitle").          [URL list](h-entry "subtitle")          A [clone](h-entry "subtitle") of request’s [URL list](h-entry "subtitle").          [initiator type](h-entry "subtitle")          "`fetch`".             
-       If init [is not empty](h-entry "subtitle"), then:  


1.         If request’s [mode](h-entry "subtitle") is   "`navigate`", then set it to "`same-origin`".   

2.         Unset request’s [reload-navigation flag](h-entry "subtitle").   

3.         Unset request’s [history-navigation flag](h-entry "subtitle").   

4.         Set request’s [origin](h-entry "subtitle") to "`client`".   

5.         Set request’s [referrer](h-entry "subtitle") to "`client`".   

6.         Set request’s [referrer policy](h-entry "subtitle") to the empty string.   

7.         Set request’s [URL](h-entry "subtitle") to request’s   [current URL](h-entry "subtitle").   

8.         Set request’s [URL list](h-entry "subtitle") to « request’s   [URL](h-entry "subtitle") ».  


This is done to ensure that when a service worker "redirects" a request, e.g., from  an image in a cross-origin style sheet, and makes modifications, it no longer appears to come from  the original source (i.e., the cross-origin style sheet), but instead from the service worker that  "redirected" the request. This is important as the original source might not even be able to  generate the same kind of requests as the service worker. Services that trust the original source  could therefore be exploited were this not done, although that is somewhat farfetched.

-       If init["`[referrer](h-entry "subtitle")`"] [exists](h-entry "subtitle"), then:  


1.         Let referrer be init["`[referrer](h-entry "subtitle")`"].   

2.         If referrer is the empty string, then set request’s   [referrer](h-entry "subtitle") to "`no-referrer`".   

3.         Otherwise:    


1.           Let parsedReferrer be the result of [parsing](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")     referrer with baseURL.     

2.           If parsedReferrer is failure, then [throw](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`.     

3.           If one of the following is true      
4.             parsedReferrer’s [scheme](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`about`" and       [path](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is the string "`client`"       

5.             parsedReferrer’s [origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is not [same origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") with       origin      

then set request’s [referrer](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to "`client`".     

6.           Otherwise, set request’s [referrer](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to     parsedReferrer.    


-       
If init["`[referrerPolicy](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`"] [exists](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then set
request’s [referrer policy](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to it.

-       
Let mode be init["`[mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`"] if
it [exists](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), and fallbackMode
otherwise.

-       
If mode is "`navigate`", then [throw](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css
"subtitle")`.

-       
If mode is non-null, set request’s [mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to
mode.

-       
If init["`[credentials](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`"] [exists](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then set
request’s [credentials mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to it.

-       
If init["`[cache](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`"] [exists](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then set
request’s [cache mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to it.

-       
If request’s [cache mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is "`only-if-cached`"
and request’s [mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") is *not*
"`same-origin`", then [throw](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css
"subtitle")`.

-       
If init["`[redirect](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`"] [exists](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle"), then set
request’s [redirect mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle") to it.

-       
If init["`[integrity](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`"] [exists](# "subtitle"), then set
request’s [integrity metadata](# "subtitle") to it.

-       
If init["`[keepalive](# "subtitle")`"] [exists](# "subtitle"), then set request’s [keepalive](# "subtitle") to it.

-       
If init["`[method](# "subtitle")`"] [exists](# "subtitle"), then:  


1.         Let method be init["`[method](# "subtitle")`"].   

2.         If method is not a [method](# "subtitle") or method is a   [forbidden method](# "subtitle"), then [throw](# "subtitle") a `[TypeError](# "subtitle")`.   

3.         [Normalize](# "subtitle") method.   

4.         Set request’s [method](# "subtitle") to method.  


-       
If init["`[signal](# "subtitle")`"] [exists](# "subtitle"), then set signal to it.

-       
If init["`[priority](# "subtitle")`"] [exists](# "subtitle"), then:  


1.         If request’s [internal priority](# "subtitle") is not null, then update   request’s [internal priority](# "subtitle") in an [implementation-defined](# "subtitle")   manner.   

2.         Otherwise, set request’s [priority](# "subtitle") to   init["`[priority](# "subtitle")`"].  


-       
Set [this](# "subtitle")’s [request](# "subtitle") to request.

-       
Let signals be « signal » if signal is non-null; otherwise « ».

-             
Set [this](# "subtitle")’s [signal](# "subtitle") to the result of [creating a dependent abort signal](# "subtitle") from
signals, using `[AbortSignal](# "subtitle")` and [this](# "subtitle")’s [relevant realm](# "subtitle").

-       
Set [this](# "subtitle")’s [headers](# "subtitle") to a [new](# "subtitle") `[Headers](# "subtitle")`
object with [this](# "subtitle")’s [relevant realm](# "subtitle"), whose [header list](# "subtitle") is
request’s [header list](# "subtitle") and [guard](# "subtitle") is "`request`".

-       
If [this](# "subtitle")’s [request](# "subtitle")’s [mode](# "subtitle") is  "`no-cors`",
then:  


1.         If [this](# "subtitle")’s [request](# "subtitle")’s [method](# "subtitle") is not a   [CORS-safelisted method](# "subtitle"), then [throw](# "subtitle") a `[TypeError](# "subtitle")`.   

2.         Set [this](# "subtitle")’s [headers](# "subtitle")’s [guard](# "subtitle") to   "`request-no-cors`".  


-       
If init [is not empty](# "subtitle"), then:  

The headers are sanitized as they might contain headers that are not allowed by this
mode. Otherwise, they were previously sanitized or are unmodified since they were
set by a  privileged API.  


1.         Let headers be a copy of [this](# "subtitle")’s [headers](# "subtitle") and its   associated [header list](# "subtitle").   

2.         If init["`[headers](# "subtitle")`"] [exists](# "subtitle"), then set   headers to init["`[headers](# "subtitle")`"].   

3.         Empty [this](# "subtitle")’s [headers](# "subtitle")’s [header list](# "subtitle").   

4.         If headers is a `[Headers](# "subtitle")` object, then [for each](# "subtitle")   header of its [header list](# "subtitle"), [append](# "subtitle")   header to [this](# "subtitle")’s [headers](# "subtitle").   

5.         Otherwise, [fill](# "subtitle") [this](# "subtitle")’s [headers](# "subtitle") with   headers.  


-       
Let inputBody be input’s [request](# "subtitle")’s [body](# "subtitle") if input
is a `[Request](# "subtitle")` object; otherwise null.

-       
If either init["`[body](# "subtitle")`"] [exists](# "subtitle") and is non-null or inputBody
is non-null, and request’s [method](# "subtitle") is \``GET`\` or \``HEAD`\`, then [throw](# "subtitle") a `[TypeError](#
"subtitle")`.

-       
Let initBody be null.

-       
If init["`[body](# "subtitle")`"] [exists](# "subtitle") and is non-null, then:  


1.         Let bodyWithType be the result of [extracting](# "subtitle")   init["`[body](# "subtitle")`"], with [*keepalive*](# "subtitle")   set to request’s [keepalive](# "subtitle").   

2.         Set initBody to bodyWithType’s [body](# "subtitle").   

3.         Let type be bodyWithType’s [type](# "subtitle").   

4.         If type is non-null and [this](# "subtitle")’s [headers](# "subtitle")’s   [header list](# "subtitle") [does not contain](# "subtitle")   \``Content-Type`\`, then [append](# "subtitle") (\``Content-Type`\`,   type) to [this](# "subtitle")’s [headers](# "subtitle").  


-       
Let inputOrInitBody be initBody if it is non-null; otherwise inputBody.

-       
If inputOrInitBody is non-null and inputOrInitBody’s  [source](# "subtitle") is null, then:  


1.         If initBody is non-null and init["`[duplex](# "subtitle")`"] does   not [exist](# "subtitle"), then throw a `[TypeError](# "subtitle")`.   

2.         If [this](# "subtitle")’s [request](# "subtitle")’s [mode](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") is neither   "`same-origin`" nor "`cors`", then throw a `[TypeError](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")`.   

3.         Set [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s   [use-CORS-preflight flag](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").  


-       
Let finalBody be inputOrInitBody.

-       
If initBody is null and inputBody is non-null, then:  


1.         If inputBody is [unusable](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"), then [throw](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")`.   

2.         Set finalBody to the result of [creating a proxy](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") for   inputBody.  


-       
Set [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s [body](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") to
finalBody.


The *`method`* getter steps are to return [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s[request](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s [method](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").

The *`url`* getter steps are to return [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s[request](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s [URL](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle"), [serialized](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").

The *`headers`* getter steps are to return[this](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle")’s [headers](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").

The *`destination`* getter are to return [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")’s[request](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")’s [destination](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle").


The *`referrer`* getter steps are:


1.       If [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")’s [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")’s [referrer](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") is "`no-referrer`", then return the empty string.

2.       If [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")’s [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")’s [referrer](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle") is "`client`", then return "`about:client`".

3.       Return [this](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")’s [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")’s [referrer](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle"), [serialized](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle").


The *`referrerPolicy`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [referrer policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

The *`mode`* getter steps are to return [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s[request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

The *`credentials`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [credentials mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

The *`cache`* getter steps are to return [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s[request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [cache mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

The *`redirect`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [redirect mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

The *`integrity`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [integrity metadata](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

The *`keepalive`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [keepalive](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

The *`isReloadNavigation`* getter steps are to returntrue if [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [reload-navigation flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is set;otherwise false.

The *`isHistoryNavigation`* getter steps are to returntrue if [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [history-navigation flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") isset; otherwise false.

The *`signal`* getter steps are to return [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s[signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

[This](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is always initialized in the[constructor](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") and when[cloning](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

The *`duplex`* getter steps are to return"`half`".

---


The *`clone()`* method steps are:


1.       If [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is [unusable](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), then [throw](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.

2.       Let clonedRequest be the result of [cloning](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

3.       [Assert](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"): [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is non-null.

4.             Let clonedSignal be the result of [creating a dependent abort signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") from « [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") », using `[AbortSignal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` and [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [relevant realm](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

5.       Let clonedRequestObject be the result of [creating](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object, given clonedRequest, [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [guard](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), clonedSignal and [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [relevant realm](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle").

6.       Return clonedRequestObject.


### 5.5. Response class

```[[Exposed](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues "You can also press the 'y'
key")=(Window,Worker)]interface *p-summary

[error](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3")();  [[NewObject](#url "Content Security Policy Level 3")]

[json](#http-cache-partitions "Content Security Policy Level 3")([any](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3") *secno
data*, optional [ResponseInit](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3") *secno

[USVString](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") [url](#general "Content Security Policy Level 3");

includes [Body](#http-network-or-cache-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3");dictionary 
*secno
ResponseInit* {  [unsigned short](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") *content
status* = 200;  [ByteString](#deferred-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") *toc
